15. tiyākarāya mutaliyār
iṅkē vē. tiyākarāya mutaliyār niṉaivu tōṉṟiya pōtē, calampūvoṭu tūpa maṟantaṟiyēṉ tamiḻōṭicai pāṭal maṟantaṟiyēṉ - nalantīṅkilum uṉṉai maṟantaṟiyēṉ uṉnāmam eṉnāvil maṟantaṟiyēṉ eṉṉum appar tirup
pāṭṭun toṭarntu tōṉṟiyatu.  kāraṇam eṉṉa?  kāraṇa
miṉṟik kāriyamillai.  appar nalantīṅkiluñ civaperumāṉai maṟantārillai.  tiyākarāyarum nalantīṅkilum eṉṉai maṟappatillai.  










 eṉṉum pāṭal eṉ niṉaivil tōṉṟiyatu 

kuḻantaimai eṉakkut teriyum.  avarkku srī pālacuppiramaṇiya paktajaṉa capaiyiṉ toṭarpu piḷḷai
maiyilēyē ēṟpaṭṭatu.  accapaiyiṉ mutal poruṭ
kāppāḷar, eṉ āruyirt tōḻar ēkāmpara mutaliyār.  avar tiyākarāyariṉ periya tantaiyār putalvar.  ēkāmparattiṉmītu yāṉ koṇṭa aṉpu tiyākarāyarai srī pālacuppiramaṇiya paktajaṉa capaiyiṉ poruṭkāppāḷarākkiyatu.
tiyākarāya mutaliyāraippaṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ cilavaṟṟai innūṟkaṇ vēṟu cilaviṭaṅkaḷiluñ colliyuḷḷēṉ;  īṇṭuñ cila kūṟa virumpukiṟēṉ. 
tiyākarāyar eṉpāl pūṇṭuḷḷa aṉpukku aḷavu uṇṭā?  illaiyā?  avvaṉpu avarai vaṉtoṇṭa
rākkiyatā?  meṉtoṇṭarākkiyatā?
muttu mutali vītiyilē īcuraṉ kōvilukkuriya vīṭu oṉṟu uṇṭu.  muṉṉāḷil pala tiṟattavar kuṭiyiruppukkeṉṟu avvīṭu payaṉpaṭṭu vantatu.  avvīṭu ōlattaiyō kūk
kuralaiyō cantaṭiyaiyō kāṇāta nāḷ irātu.  orunāḷ iraṇṭu kuṭiyarkkuḷ caṇṭai mūṇṭatu.  caṇṭai oruvarai oruvar kiṇaṟṟil tūkkiviṭum aḷavu perukiyatu.  accamayam avvīṭṭil kūkkural eḻuntatu.  atu teruvaiyē tiraṭṭiyatu.  annilaiyil yāṉ pōlisukku āḷ aṉuppiṉēṉ.  aḵtu oru mūpparukkut terintatu.  avar eṉṉaik kaṇṭatuñ cīṟiṉar.  ac cīṟṟam pakkattilirunta tiyākarāyariṉ iḷaṅkaiyai erittatu.  avveri ammūpparmītu viḻuntatu.  eḻunta aṉalait taṇikka nīṇṭa nēramāyiṟṟu.
ōr iḷaiñaṉ eṉṉiṭam takutittāḷ peṟa muyaṉṟāṉ.  avaṉ takutittāṭku uriyavaṉallaṉ.  avaṉatu muyaṟci eṉṉai iṇaṅkac ceyyavillai.  avaṉ eṉṉai uṟṟu nōkki, pārttuk koḷkiṟēṉ eṉṟāṉ.  avaṉ collai yāṉ poruṭpaṭuttavillai.  orunāḷ terukkōṭiyil avaṉ kāṇappaṭṭāṉ.  avaṉ kāṭci eṉakku aiyamūṭṭiyatu.  aiyam eṉṉai neṭukac cellaviṭavillai.  maṟṟorunāḷ naḷ
ḷiravil vīṭṭut terut tiṇṇaiyil aravaṅ kēṭṭatu.  yāṉ palakaṇi vaḻiyē pārttēṉ.  koṭiyaṉ uruvam!  yāṉ viḷak
kēṟṟiyatum avaṉ ōṭiviṭṭāṉ.  icceytiyai yāṉ cilariṭañ coṉṉēṉ.  atu tiyākarāyarukkut teriya vantatu.  avviḷaiñaṉ orunāḷ muttu mutali vīti vaḻiyē ceṉṟāṉ.  tiyākarāyar avaṉukku eccarikkai vaḻaṅkiṉār.  avaṉ iṟutta viṭai avaṉukku utaiyāyiṟṟu.
irāyappēṭṭai pāṅkiyilē oru vakkīlukkum eṉakkum orupōtu vākkuvātam naṭantatu.  vakkīl varampu kaṭanta vēḷaiyil eṉṉuṭaṉirunta tiyākarāyar mūccu avaraik kīḻē taḷḷiyatu.
tiruvallikkēṇic civaṉaṭiyār tirukkūṭṭattilē āṟāṇṭu yāṉ periya purāṇap piracaṅkañ ceytēṉ.  tiyākarāyar appiracaṅkaṅ kēṭpatil tavaṟuvatillai.  yāṉ eṭuttukkāṭṭum mēṟkōḷkaḷ palavum avarukku maṉappāṭamāyiṉa.
tiyākarāya mutaliyār mutal mutal vēlai tuvaṅkiya iṭam hikkiṉpātam aṇṭ kampeṉi.  yāṉ tēcapaktaṉ nilaiyattukku aḻaittēṉ;  aṅkē avar accup piḻai tirut
tum payiṟci peṟṟup piṉṉē attuṟaiyil vallavarāṉār.  yāṉ navacakti āciriyaṉākiyapōtu, cakti nilaiyam tiyākarāyarai aḻaiyātirukkumō?  navacakti nilaiyattil tiyākarāyarai accuppiḻai tiruttum aḷavil niṟuttiṉē
ṉillai;  ceyti tiraṭṭuvatilum payiṟci peṟumāṟu viṭuttēṉ.  atilum avar maṇiyāyiṉar.
tiyākarāya mutaliyār eṉ pēccaik kēṭṭum eṉ eḻuttil ūṟiyum nalla eḻuttāḷarāyiṉār.
ñāṉiyār cuvāmikaḷ orumuṟai ceṅkaṟpaṭṭil taṅkiya
pōtu tiyākarāyar eṉakkoru muṭaṅkal tīṭṭiṉar.  ataik kaṇṭa cuvāmikaḷ tiyākarāyaraip pārttu, nīr yāriṭam tamiḻ payiṉṟīr eṉṟu kēṭṭaṉarām.  tiyākarāyar, yāṉ entap paḷḷikkum pōntatillai;  evariṭattum tamiḻ payiṉṟatillai.  eṉ paḷḷi tiru. vi. ka. vē. avar pēccum eḻuttum yāṉ payiṉṟavai eṉṟu patil kūṟiṉārām.
muṉṉāḷil yāṉ teṉṉāṭu nōkkumpōtellām tiruppātirippuliyūrai maṟappatillai.  orumuṟai ñāṉiyār cuvāmikaḷuṭaṉ uraiyāṭiya vēḷaiyil, cuvāmikaḷ ceṅkaṟ
paṭṭu nikaḻcciyai naviṉṟu, kampaṉ vīṭṭuk kaṭṭuttaṟiyum kavipāṭum eṉṉum moḻi tiyākarāyarāl meymmaiya
tāyiṟṟu eṉṟār.
tiyākarāya mutaliyār kūrtta mati eṉṉuḷḷaṅ kavarum.  avar piḷḷaimaiyil ikkālap paḷḷiyil payiṉṟiruppā
rāyiṉ, avar oru paṭṭatāriyāy oru perum pataviyil vīṟṟiruppar eṉpatil aiyamillai.  namatu nāṭṭil et
taṉaiyō maṇikaḷ eḷimai kāraṇamākat takkaviṭattil oḷikālātu eṅkeṅkēyō maṅkik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.
tiyākarāyariṉ pāṭṭaṉāraiyum, avar vaḻittōṉṟiya vīrap putalvarkaḷaiyum yāṉ aṟivēṉ.  avaratu celvamum celvākkum irāyappēṭṭaiyai āṭcipurinta kālamum uṇṭu.  tiyākarāyariṉ uṇmai, añcāmai mutaliya paṇpukaḷ avartam kuṭumpattiṉ toṉmaiyai mīṭṭum uyirppittaṉa.  itaṉāl viḷainta payaṉait tiyākarāyar tamakkē urimai
yākkik koḷḷāmal, tamatu uṭaṉ piṟantār nalaṉukkum urimaiyākkiya paṇpu eṉṉai uvakkac ceyyum.  tiyākarāya
ruṭaṉ piṟantavar mūvar.  oruvar tamaiyaṉār;  maṟṟiruvar tampimār.  tamaiyaṉār civāṉanta mutaliyār cātu;  āṉāl vīrar.  oru tampi turaicāmi eṉpavar accut toḻilil vallunarāki ōraccaka urimaiyāḷarā yiruttalum, maṟṟoru tampi irattiṉa meṉpavar aracāṅkak kūṭṭuṟavu ilākāvil naṟṟoṇṭāṟṟic ciṟappuṭaṉ vāḻtalum pāṭṭaṉār kumāracāmi mutaliyāriṉ kuṭumpa māṇpai nilaipeṟutti varukiṉṟaṉa.  ammūvarum eṉakku iṉiyar.
veṟuṅ kūrtta matimaṭṭum oruvar vāḻkkaiyaip paṇpaṭuttuvatākātu.  ataṉuṭaṉ piṟarkkutavum pēṟum amaintiruttal vēṇṭum.  appōtē vāḻkkai, poṉ
malar nāṟṟamuṭaittākum.  tiyākarāyariṉ kūrttamati maṭṭum eṉakku makiḻcciyūṭṭuvatillai;  avaratu oppuravum eṉakku makiḻcciyūṭṭum.
eṅkaḷ vīṭṭu nalviṉai tīviṉaikaḷil peruṅkūṭṭam tiraḷum.  apperuṅ kūṭṭattukku vēṇṭuva ceytaliṉ arumaippāṭṭai viḷakkat tēvaiyillai.  javaḷi naṭēca mutaliyārum tiyākarāya mutaliyārum oruvarāy eṅkaḷ arumaippāṭṭai eḷitākkum oppuravai yāṉ orumuṟaiyā kaṇṭirukkiṟēṉ?  paṉmuṟai kaṇṭirukkiṟēṉ.  vēṟu cilac cila iṭaṅkaḷilum tiyākarāyar oppuravu ulavivarum.

ṭāvatu iyalpu.  irāyappēṭṭaiyilum palatiṟap piṇakkukaḷ eḻum.  appiṇakkukaḷait tīrppatil tiyākarāyar toṇṭum pukum.  avar paṇi, kuḻappaṅkaḷilum ūṭuruvit taṇikkai ceyyum.  uyirkkiṟuti nērumō eṉṟa aiyameḻunta ōriṭattil tiyākarāyar cēvai payaṉpaṭṭatu.  ivai civaṉaṭiyārkkuriya paṇpukaḷ eṉṟu yāṉ pārāṭṭuvēṉ.
tiyākarāyar kaṇṭippāḷar eṉṟoru peyar peṟṟirukkiṟār.  avar tam kaṇṭippu eṅku nikaḻumeṉpatai yāṉaṟivēṉ.  taṉṉalattālum cerukkāluṅ kuṭumpaṅkaṭkum potunalaṅkaṭkun tīṅku cūḻvōriṭattut tiyākarāyariṉ kaṇṭippu eḻuntētīrum.  atai eṉ uḷḷam pōṟṟum.  tiyākarāyariṉ kaṇṭippum, nērmaiyum avaratu naṉmatippukkuk kālkoṇṭatōṭu potu amaippu nirvākaṅkaḷilum avarai niṟuttiṉa eṉṟu curuṅkac colkiṟēṉ.  tiyāka
rāyarkkuc cīrtirutta maṉappāṉmaiyuṇṭu.
toṭakkattil pālacuppiramaṇiya paktajaṉa capaiyiṉ āṇṭu viḻākkaḷ eṉ paṇiyai ēṟṟaṉa.  annāḷil capai irāyappēṭṭaiyiṉ akattiliruntatu.  ataṉāl pēṭṭai makkaḷ tuṇai eḷitil kiṭaikkum.  ippoḻutu capai irāyappēṭṭaiyiṉ puṟattē irukkiṟatu.  pēṭṭai makkaḷ tuṇaiyum perumitamākak kiṭaippatillai.  cila camayam āṇṭu viḻākkaḷ eppaṭi naṭaipeṟum eṉṟu yāṉ ēkkuṟu
vēṉ.  eṉatu ēkkam tiyākarāya mutaliyāriṉ ūkkattālum uḻaippālum nīṅkum.  avaratu nirvākattiṟam ellāvaṟṟai
yuñ cīrceyyum.

16. kuppucāmi mutaliyār
eṉakkiṉiya patikaḷuḷ kāñciyum oṉṟu.  kāñci eṉakku vaḻaṅkuṅ kāṭci palatiṟam.  avvakkāñci niṉaiviluṟuṅkāl ciṟcila naṇpar mukaṅkaḷ eṉ cintaiyil ceṟiyum.  civa

1908ām āṇṭu naṇpar civacaṅkara mutaliyārum yāṉum kāñciyil naṭakkiṟōm; vaṇṭiyil celkiṟōm;  tiruk kōyilkaḷil pukukiṟōm;  yāṉ coṟpoḻivāṟṟukiṟēṉ.  eṅkaḷai ōr iḷamai toṭarkiṟatu.  nīr niḻaleṉat toṭar kiṟatu.  eṉatu periya purāṇa mutaṟ patippuc cañcikaiyaip paṟṟiya pēccē atikam.  aṉṟu eṉṉuḷ paṭinta nelluk kāratteru nēyam iṉṟum vaḷarntu varukiṟatu.  annēyamē eṉatu kāñcit tōṟṟam.  aṉṟu eṅkaḷait toṭarnta iḷamaiyar - iṉiyar - yār?  iṉṟu kumaraṉ accaka urimai yāḷar - kumāravikaṭaṉ āciriyar - vaḷḷuvar viruntai ōmpi varuvōr-tavaci kuppucāmi mutaliyār.

17. irāmaliṅkam
yāṉ veli kallūriyil tamiḻt toṇṭāṟṟiya kālattil eṉatu uḷḷaṅkoṇṭa māṇākkar cilar.  avaruḷ oruvar kī. irāmaliṅka mutaliyār.  irāmaliṅkam aṭakkattuk
kōr uṟaiyuḷ eṉṟu aṉṟu kaṇṭēṉ;  iṉṟum avariṭam ataiyē kāṇkiṟēṉ.  irāmaliṅkam māṇākkarāyirunta
pōtē avarait tamiḻk kātal maṇantatu.  akkātal avarait tamiḻ em.ē. ākkiyatu.  avar piṟa moḻic coṟkaḷukkut tamiḻ kāṇpatil kaṇṇuṅ karuttumuṭaiyavar.  piṟa moḻic coṟkaḷāl tamiḻ aṉṉaikku ammainōy uṇṭātal kūṭāteṉpatu avaratu koḷkai.

18. vicuvanātaṉ
1924ām āṇṭu yāṉ tūttukkuṭi nōkkiṉēṉ;  tiriccip pukaivaṇṭi nilaiyattil kē.e.pi vicuvanātaṉaik kaṇṭēṉ.  avaratu ceḻumai koḻitta iḷamaiyum, neṟṟi
yil nīṟum, kaḻuttil maṇi mālaiyum, malarnta mukamum kaṇṇukku iṉimai yūṭṭiṉa.  avar oru tuṇṭu veḷiyīṭṭai nīṭṭiṉar.  atai aṅkum iṅkum pārttēṉ.  ataṉkaṇ kiṟituvar ceyyum matamāṟṟattaip paṟṟiya maṟuppukaḷ malintiruntaṉa.  caivarkaḷ eṉ ceykiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu kēṭ
ṭēṉ.  caivarkaḷ tiricciyil ceyyum vēlaikaḷ avarāl viḷakkappaṭṭaṉa.  vaṇṭi puṟappaṭṭatu.  mīṇṭum iḷaiñarait tūttukkuṭiyil pārttēṉ.  aṅkum avarāl tuṇṭu veḷiyīṭukaḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa.
vicuvanātaṉ tiricciyil caivattoṇṭu āṟṟukiṟār eṉṟu cōlṭaṉ accaka urimaiyāḷarum, eṉatu paḻaiya naṇparumākiya pālacuntara mutaliyār vāyilākak kēḷviyuṟu
vēṉ;  piṉṉē avar jaṭi kaṭciyil cērntaṉar eṉṟu aṟintēṉ.  nāyakkar iyakkattilum avar maṉañ ceṉṟatu.
nāyakkarum ṭākṭar varatarājaluvum yāṉum araciyalil māṟupaṭṭa kālattu, mūvaraiyum tiricciyil oṉṟupaṭutta vēṇṭumeṉṟa viruppam vicuvanātaṉ uḷḷattil tavaḻnta
teṉṟu teṉṟal vāyilāka aṟintēṉ.  vicuvanātaṉ viruppattukku nāyakkar iṇaṅkār eṉṟu atteṉṟaliṭamē colli viṭuttēṉ.
nāyakkariṉ iyakkattukku ākkan tēṭuvatil vicuvanātaṉ cintai ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyatu.  appoḻutu, tiricci iḷamai pāḻukku uḻaikkiṟatu eṉṟu yāṉ niṉaintēṉ.  vicuvanātaṉ ovvorupōtu ceṉṉaiyil eṉṉaik kāṇpar.  karuttu vēṟṟumai avar maṉattil kāḻppaip pukuttuvatillai eṉpatu eṉakku naṉku viḷaṅkiyatu.  avviyalpai eṉ maṉam pōṟṟum.
hintik kiḷarcci vīṟiṭṭatu.  nāyakkar ciṟai pukuntār.  avvēḷaiyil akkiḷarcciyai vicuvanātaṉ naṭāttiya muṟaiyum oḻuṅkum nērmaiyum tiṟamum eṉṉai viyakkac ceytaṉa.  vicuvanātaṉ ōr iyakkattukkut talaimai pūṇṭu atai naṭāttum āṟṟaluṭaiyavar eṉpatu nāṭṭukkuc cevvaṉē pulappaṭṭatu.  avartam araciyal koḷkai eṉ vāḻttukku uriyatākavillai.  āṉāl avaratu oḻuṅkum nērmaiyum tiṟamum eṉ vāḻttukku uriyaṉavāyiṉa.
vicuvanātaṉ uṭalil iṉṉum iḷamaiyē tavaḻkiṟatu.  avar vakuppurimai uṇarviṉiṉṟum viṭutalaiyaṭaintu, nāṭṭurimaiyil īṭupaṭuṅ kālam varum eṉṟu etir nōkku
vōruḷ yāṉum oruvaṉ.  vicuvanātaṉ pōṉṟōr uḻaippu nāṭṭurimai iyakkattukkup payaṉpaṭal vēṇṭumeṉpatu eṉatu viruppam.  āṇṭavaṉ aruḷ ceyvāṉāka.
19. nārāyaṇacāmi nāyuṭu
kiḻakkumarutūr nārāyaṇacāmi nāyuṭuviṉ kēṇmai eṉakku eppoḻutu kiṭaittatu?  avar māṇākkarāyirunta ñāṉṟē kiṭaittatu.
tiruppātirippuliyūrilē ñāṉiyār maṭattilē eṉatu coṟpoḻivu aṭikkaṭi naṭanta kālam uṇṭu.  orupōtu eṉ pēccu muṭintatu.  kūṭṭaṅ kalaintatu.  kai kāl cuttañ ceyya eṇṇi otuṅki naṭantēṉ.  oru ciṟiya melliya vaṭivam eṉmuṉ tōṉṟiyatu.  avvaṭiviṉar eṉṉuṭaṉ uraiyāṭa viraital pulaṉāyiṟṟu.  avar, yāṉ mūṉṟāvatu pāra māṇāk
kaṉ.  eṉ peyar nārāyaṇacāmi eṉṟār. evvaḷavu nēram iṅkē iruntīr eṉṟu kēṭṭēṉ;  uṅkaḷ pēccu muḻuvatuṅ kēṭṭiruntēṉ eṉṟa patil peṟṟēṉ.   eṉṉa!  ikkāla māṇākkar!  mūṉṟām pāra māṇākkar!  camayap pēccai ivvaḷavu nēraṅ kēṭṭaṉar eṉṟu niṉaintēṉ;  avarai uṟṟu uṟṟu nōkkiṉēṉ;  avar mukattil kalaik katir kāṉ
ṟatu kaṇṭēṉ.  ciṟitunēram uraiyāṭi avar viṭaipeṟṟuc ceṉṟār.

nārāyaṇacāmiyiṉ aṉpukku iṇaṅki avartam tantaiyār uruvappaṭattait tiṟakkumporuṭṭu yāṉ marutūrkkuc ceṉṟēṉ.  marutūr marutap paṇṇākavē kāṭciyaḷittatu.  keṭilamum ataṉ veḷḷiya maṇaṟparappum eṉ maṉattaik koḷḷai koṇṭaṉa.  aṅkē oru kuṭil amaittut taṅkiviṭalāmā eṉṟum eṉ neñcam eṇṇiyatu.

20. taṇṭapāṇi piḷḷai
citamparam eṉ.  taṇṭapāṇi piḷḷaiyaiyum eṉṉaiyum toṭarpupaṭuttiyatu araciyaṟṟuṟai.  attoṭarpāl viḷainta nikaḻccikaḷ innūṟkaṇ āṅkāṅkē tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.  ātaliṉ, aṅkē eñciya iraṇṭoṉṟaik kuṟittal cālum.

paktaṉil viḷakkik kāṭṭiṉēṉ.

kaḷil nakaiccuvai maliyap pēcuvār.  avar viḷaiyāṭalkaḷ pala niṉaivukku varukiṉṟaṉa.

1920ām āṇṭil taṇṭapāṇi ceṉṉaic ciṟaiyi
ṉiṉṟum eṉakkoru kaṭitam viṭuttaṉar.  ciṟai narakam;  mūttira nāṟṟam.  nīṅkaḷ vārātēyuṅkaḷ eṉṟa kuṟippu akkaṭi
tattiliruntatu.  iraṇṭoru tiṅkaḷ kaṭantu kūṭalūrc ciṟaiyiṉiṉṟum avar tīṭṭiya muṭaṅkal vēṟu vitamāyiruntatu.  atil kūṭalūrcciṟai naṉṟāyirukkiṟatu.  nam varatar iruk kiṟār.  ellām kiṭaikkum vāruṅkaḷ eṉṟa kuṟippuk kulavi iruntatu.
taṇṭapāṇi ciṟaikkōṭṭattiṉiṉṟum veḷivanta pōtu, yāṉ navacaktiyil (02.12.1921) naṉmoḻi kūṟiṉēṉ.  atil orupakuti varumāṟu:-
ematarumai naṇpa!  taṇṭapāṇi!  intiyap pātukāppuc cēṉaiyil cērnta vīra!  naṭpōṭu, taṇṭapāṇiyōṭu, vīrat taṉmaiyōṭu tēca cēvai ceytīr.  umatu iḷamparuvattai - arumai aṉṉaiyai - maintaṉai - maṉaiviyai - cakōtararai maṟantu avarmītu koṇṭa paṟṟai - vēṭkaiyai - kātalai - aṉpaip pāratamātāviṉmītu celuttic ciṟai ceṉṟīr - nīr puṉitarāṉīr.  ummaip pōṟṟukiṉṟōm.
taṇṭapāṇi kāṅkirasiliruntār;  jaṭisil pukun
tār;  cuyamariyātaiyil cērntār.  immāṟutal palarkkup palavitak karuttukkaḷai uṇṭupaṇṇi yirukkum.  yāṉ taṇṭapāṇiyiṉ uḷḷattai uṇarntavaṉ.  avar ekkaṭciyil nuḻaintālum avar maṉam nāṭṭu viṭutalaiyai maṟappatillai eṉpatu eṉakkut teriyum.  ātaliṉ, taṇṭapāṇi ennilaiyil niṉṟālum evvēṭaṅkoṇṭālum yāṉ kavalaiyuṟuva
tillai.  avar viṭutalaip pōr vīrar eṉpatu tēṟṟam.  iṉṟu uṇmaip pōr tuvaṅkappaṭiṉ, ataṉ muṉṉaṇiyil taṇṭapāṇi niṟpar.
21. mācilāmaṇi mutaliyār
mācilāmaṇi mutaliyārai yāṉ iḷamai toṭṭu aṟivēṉ.  avartan toṇṭu ceṉṉaic civaṉaṭiyār tirukkūṭṭattil toṭaṅkiyatu.  piṉṉē attoṇṭu taṇṭucīvarameṉṉum vālājāpāttil pālāṟṟaṅkaraiyil intumatap pāṭacālaiyāyp perukiyatu.  mācilāmaṇi mutaliyāriṉ vāḻkkai appaḷḷikkeṉṟē arppaṇamā
kiyatu.  appaḷḷikkeṉṟē avar vāḻkiṟār.
intumatap pāṭacālai mēlum mēlum ōṅkutal vēṇṭumeṉṟu niṉaippavaruḷ yāṉum oruvaṉ.  veṟum niṉaippu eṉ ceyyum?  paḷḷikkup poruḷutavi tēvai.  ēḻaiyēṉ eṉ ceyyavallēṉ!  mācilāmaṇi mutaliyār eṉṉai aṭikkaṭi tamatu paḷḷikku aḻaippār.  ēḻai varukaiyāl umatu paḷḷikku eṉṉa nalaṉ viḷaiyappōkiṟatu?  btW« thœ¤J v‹brŒí«? eṉṟu colli colli yāṉ maṟuppu vaḻaṅkuvatuṇṭu.  uṅkaḷ varukaiyāl nalaṉ viḷaiyum eṉṟu mutaliyār vāy navilum.  ciṟcila camayam avartam paḷḷi pōntu atai vāḻtti varuvēṉ.
orumuṟai kalki āciriyar rā. kiruṣṇamūrttiyuṭaṉ yāṉ ceṉṟu tirumpiyatāl nalaṉ viḷaintatu eṉṟu mutali
yār kūṟak kēṭṭēṉ.
mācilāmaṇi mutaliyār toṇṭu paḷḷiyaḷavil niṟpatillai;  vevvēṟiṭaṅkaḷiluñ cellum.  tamiḻ nāṭṭil naṭaipeṟum palavakai viḻākkaḷilum ciṟappukkaḷilum mācilāmaṇi mutaliyāraik kāṇalām.  avviṭaṅkaḷil toṇṭu nikaḻuṅ kūṭṭaṅkaḷil mutaliyārai yāṉ kaṇṭatum, toṇṭar pōntuḷḷār.  evvitak kuṟaivum uṟātu eṉṟu eṉ neñcam niṉaikkum.  mācilāmaṇi mutaliyār muyaṟci velka.
22. cuppiramaṇiyāccāriyār
viḻuppurattukku aṇittē cila kirāmaṅkaḷirukkiṉṟaṉa.  avaikaḷuḷ oṉṟu veṅkantūr.  avvūril mācimakantoṟum āmāttūr perumāṉ eḻuntaruḷvatu vaḻakkam.  annāḷil aṅkē potukkūṭṭaṅ kūṭum.  1918ām āṇṭu mutal mūṉṟāṇṭu potukkūṭṭattil yāṉ pēci vantēṉ.  veṅkantūrukkum eṉakkum toṭarpu ēṟpaṭṭatu.
vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻip pulavarai īṉṟa ciṟappu veṅkantūrukku uṇṭu.  avaruḷ kuṟikkattakkavar kaṇapati cātiriyār, cōmatēva carmā, cuppiramaṇiyāccāriyār mutaliyōr.
vittuvāṉ cuppiramaṇiyāccāriyār paccaiyappaṉ kallūrit tamiḻp pērāciriyarāyt tamiḻt toṇṭu āṟṟi varukiṟār.  akkallūrit tamiḻc caṅkattil eṉatu coṟ
poḻivu cila camayam naṭakkum.  peritum tamiḻp pērāciriyar cuppiramaṇiyāccāriyārāl naṉṟi kūṟappaṭum.  annaṉṟiyil tēcapaktaṉ nilaiyattil pesaṇṭ ammaiyārum yāṉum uraiyāṭiyataiyum, ammaiyār eṉakku naṉṉūl pala aḷit
tataiyuñ ciṟappāka āccāriyār kuṟippiṭuvar.  annikaḻcci
kaḷai eṟṟukkuc cuppiramaṇiyam kuṟippiṭukiṟāreṉṟu yāṉ niṉaippēṉ.
vittuvāṉ cuppiramaṇiyāccāriyār eṉ talaimaiyil palatiṟac coṟpoḻivukaḷ nikaḻttiyuḷḷār.  avaikaḷil veṟṟuraikaḷai yāṉ kaṇṭatillai.  āccāriyāriṉ citta
rulaka ārāycci eṉakku makiḻcciyūṭṭum.  avar tam pēccāl avar oru ciṟanta uraiyāciriyar eṉpatu pulaṉāyiṟṟu.
eṉpāl pēraṉpu vāyntavaruḷ cuppiramaṇiyāccāri
yārum oruvar.  avartam aṉpiṉiṉṟum piṟakkum nalleṇ
ṇam eṉatu vāḻnāḷaiyum vaḷarkkun tiṟam vāyntateṉṟu yāṉ karutuvatuṇṭu.
23. aruḷ taṅkaiyā
yāṉ iḷamaiyil cila kiṟituvar uṟavaip peṟṟēṉ.  avarāl eṉ vāḻkkai pala vaḻiyilun tiruntiyatu.  mutumaiyil oru kiṟituvar uṟavu eṉakkuk kiṭaittatu.  avar reva
reṇṭu ā. aruḷ taṅkaiyā.
aruḷ taṅkaiyā tamiḻar;  tamiḻp pulavar;  tamiḻk kātalar.  avartam tamiḻk kātal hintik kiḷarcciyiṉ pōtu nāṭṭukkut teriyalāyiṟṟu.  avvēḷaiyil aruḷ taṅkaiyā eḻutiya tamiḻkkaṭṭuraikaḷ navacaktiyil veḷiyāyiṉa.  piṉṉē eṉ talaimaiyil avaratu camayac coṟpoḻivukaḷ cila nikaḻntaṉa.  akkaṭṭuraikaḷum iccoṟpoḻivukaḷum avaraiyum eṉṉaiyum uṟavupaṭuttiṉa.  aṉpar tamiḻum kiṟituvamum eṉakku iṉiyaṉavāyiṉa.  aruḷ taṅkaiyāvāl eḻutappeṟṟa oru nūlukku yāṉ oru ciṟu ciṟappurai (04.06.41) cūṭṭiṉēṉ. atu varumāṟu:-
periyatu etu?  aṉpu. aṉpu periyateṉṟu pōtaṉai
yiṉālum cātaṉaiyiṉālum ulakukkuc ciṟappāka aṟivuṟut
tiyavar evar?  kiṟituperumāṉ.  kiṟituvē aṉpu;  aṉpē kiṟitu.  
kiṟituperumāṉ vāḻkkai varalāṟu palarāl palavāṟu eḻutappaṭṭatu.  kallellām māṇikkak kallāmō?  mikac cilar eḻuttilēyē kiṟituperumāṉ aṉpu ceṟivatāyiṟṟu.
aṉpar jē. pāṟṟarsaṉ cimit eḻutiya kiṟitu perumāṉ vāḻkkai varalāṟu palavaḻiyilum ciṟappu vāyntatu.  ciṟappukkaḷai īṇṭu virittuk kūṟavēṇṭuvatillai.  jē.  pāṟṟarsaṉ cimit kiṟituperumāṉ aṉpil niṉṟavar; mūḻkiyavar;  tiḷaittavar;  oṉṟiyavar.  aṉpil oṉṟiya oruvar pēccum eḻuttum aṉpu vaṇṇamākap polital iyalpaṉṟō?  aṉpar jē.  pāṟṟarsaṉ cimittiṉ ovvōr eḻuttum aṉpiṉiṉṟum mukiḻttateṉpatu avar eḻutiyuḷḷa kiṟituperumāṉ vāḻkkai varalāṟṟil tōyntu paṭivarkku naṉku pulaṉātal orutalai.
aṉpar jē.  pāṟṟarsaṉ cimit eḻutiya aṉpuk kiṟitu
viṉ vāḻkkai varalāṟṟait tamiḻnāṭṭukku utava uyartiru. ā. aruḷ taṅkaiyā muyaṉṟaṉar.  aruḷ taṅkaiyā tamatu muyaṟciyil veṟṟi peṟṟaṉar eṉṟē kūṟalām.  aṟiñar taṅkaiyāviṉ moḻi peyarppai yāṉ pārttapōtu, itu mutal nūlā?  moḻipeyarppu nūlā? eṉṉum aiyam eṉakkut tōṉṟalāyiṟṟu.  innūlaip paṭippōr itaṉai mutal nūl eṉṟu karutum muṟaiyil moḻi
peyarpput toṇṭāṟṟiya aṟiñar aruḷ taṅkaiyāviṉ pulamaiyum, tiṟaṉum, aṉpum pōṟṟaṟkuriyaṉa.  avarukku eṉatu naṉṟi uriyatāka.
tamiḻnāṭu innūlaip poṉṉēpōl pōṟṟi ēṟpatāka.  tamiḻ velka;  tamiḻ vāḻka.
aṇittē aruḷ taṅkaiyāvaik kaṇṭēṉ;  ciṟitu nēram uraiyāṭiṉēṉ.  atupōḻtu kiṟituvattai aṭip
paṭaiyāk koṇṭa potu aṟak karuttukkaḷ avariṭat
tiruntu veḷiyāyiṉa.  yāṉ iṉpak kaṭalāṭiṉēṉ.  varuṅkāla ulakap pēṟu etu?  potu aṟamaṉṟō?
24. mīṉāṭcicuntaram piḷḷai
te. po. mīṉāṭcicuntaram piḷḷai pulavarā?  kalaiñarā?  kalaiyā?  kalaikkaḻakamā?
kuṟiñci mullai marutam neytal eṉpaṉa nālvakai nilam.  atataṟkuriya maram puḷ vilaṅku mutaliyaṉa uṇṭu;  avvāṟē makkaḷuṇṭu;  avartam vaḻakka voḻukkaṅkaḷuṇṭu;  paṇṇuṇṭu;  piṟavumuṇṭu.  ivaikaḷ iyaṟkaik kalaikaḷ.  ivaikaḷiṉiṉṟum malarntavai kāviya ōviyak kalaikaḷ.  ikkalaikaḷil tōyntu paṭintu tiḷaittu iṉpat tēṉaip paruki mayaṅkik kiṭantu, avaikaḷāy eḻuntu, pāṇmiḻaṟṟun tamiḻ vaṇṭukaḷ cila uḷḷaṉa.  avaikaḷil oṉṟu mīṉāṭci cuntaram.
te. po. mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉāraic cilar palkalaik kaḻakam eṉṟu kūṟupa.  eṉakku avar avvāṟu tōṉṟukiṟārillai.  avar palkalaikkaḻakattaiyuṅ kaṭanta oruvar eṉpatu eṉ uṭkiṭakkai.  mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār kāṭci, eṉakkuk kuṟiñciyum mullaiyum marutamum neytalum cērnta oru kaḻakamākat tōṉṟukiṟatu.
mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār uḷḷattil oru moḻiyā ūṟiyatu?  pala moḻikaḷ ūṟiṉa.  tamiḻ teluṅku malai
yāḷam āriyam hinti vaṅkam āṅkilam pirañcu jermeṉ mutaliya pala moḻikaḷ ūṟiūṟi avar vaṇṇamāyiṉa.  mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉāraic cūḻnta pala moḻi aṇaṅkukaḷai, ōr aṇaṅku viḻuṅki, avarkaḷaiyellām taṉ vaṇṇamākki viṭṭāḷ.  avaḷ evaḷ?  avaḷē tamiḻ aṇaṅku.  tamiḻ aṇaṅku mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉārai vakkīl toḻilukku viṭuvatillai;  paḷḷi āciriyat toḻilukkuc celuttuva
tillai;  vēṟu toḻilukkum untuvatilai;  avarait taṉ vayappaṭuttiṉāḷ.  cuntaraṉār tamiḻt toṇṭarāṉār.  avariṭattiliruntu tamiḻ vittuvāṉkaḷum, tamiḻ em.ēkkaḷum piṟattal iyalpē.
eṉ talaimaiyil mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār coṟpoḻivu
kaḷ pala nikaḻntuḷḷaṉa.  avaikaḷiṉ tiṟaṅkaḷai eṉṉeṉṟu varuṇippēṉ!  avar pēccil kuṟiñci neytalākum;  neytal kuṟiñciyākum;  mullai marutamākum;  marutam mullaiyākum;  oṉṟuṭaṉ oṉṟu mayaṅkum - kampar ṣēkpiyarāvar;  ṣēkpiyar cēkkiḻārāvar;  parañcōti vāṉmīkiyāvar;  vāṉmīki ñāṉacampantarāvar;  ñāṉacampantar ṣelliyāvar;  ṣelli umārāvar;  umār kettēyāvar;  ellārum vaḷḷuvarāvar;  kītāccāriyarāvar  - vētāntam cittānta
mākum;  kiṟituvam vaiṣṇavamākum;  pauttam jaina
mākum.  kaṭalnīr āviyāy, maḻaiyāy, āṟāy, mīṇṭuṅ kaṭalnīrāvatu iyaṟkai.  mīṉāṭcicuntaram kalaikkaṭal.  avar pēccil ellām oṉṟākum.  itil eṉṉa viyappu?  yāṉ potumai iṉpattai nukarntukoṇṭiruppēṉ.
mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār nakkīrar, iḷaṅkō aṭikaḷ, cāttaṉār mutaliyavaraippaṟṟip pēcuvār.  avvakkālattil avaravar avaikkaṇ koluvīṟṟiruttal eṉ akakkaḷattil poruḷākum.
cilar oru kaviñarai maṟṟoru kaviñaruṭaṉ oppiṭṭu uyarvu tāḻvu kaṟpittut tāmum iṭarppaṭuvar;  avaiyaiyum iṭarppaṭuttuvar.  mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār kaviñarkkuḷ uyarvu tāḻvu kaṟpittut tāmum iṭarppaṭuvatillai;  avaiyaiyum iṭarppaṭuttuvatillai.  niṟaikuṭam nīr taḷumpalil eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.  māvaiyum aracaiyum oppiṭṭu uyarvu tāḻvu kūṟuvatu aṟivuṭaimaiyākumā?  iraṇṭum iyaṟkaiyil tōṉṟiyaṉavē.
mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār kāviyam pēcumpōtu kāviyamāvar;  ōviyam pēcumpōtu ōviyamāvar.  avar vāyiṉiṉṟum aruvi perukip perukit tiṇ - tiṇṇeṉa vīḻntukoṇṭirukkum.  avvīḻcciyai eḻuttil iṟakkal iyalātu;  avarālum iyalātu;  maṟṟavarālum iyalātu.  aḵtu eṉakkuk kuṟṟāla viruntākum.  kāviya ōviya iṉpam nukartaṟku uriyatu;  eḻuttukku uriyataṉṟu.
mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār eḻuttum pēccup pōṉṟatē.  avartaṉ eḻutukōlil tolkāppiyamō mēsaṉō kōyilkoḷvatillai.  eḻuttu veḷḷam eppaṭi ep
paṭiyō puraṇṭu ōṭum.  avar eḻutuvatai yāṉ palamuṟai pārttirukkiṟēṉ.
yāṉ iḷamaiyil cintātirippēṭṭai vacanta maṇṭa
pattil tāyumāṉāraippaṟṟip pēciṉēṉ.  appoḻutu mīṉāṭcicuntaram ciṟṟiḷañ cēyāy aṅkē ulaviṉarām.  itai avar collavē yāṉ kēṭṭēṉ.
1924ām āṇṭil kāṅkira ceṉṉai nakaracapaiyait taṉ mayamākka muyaṉṟatu.  cintātirippēṭṭai taṉatu mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉārai viḻaintatu.  appēṭṭaiyil kāṅkira cārpil kūṭiya kūṭṭattil yāṉ pēciṉēṉ.  mīṉāṭci
cuntaraṉārum pēciṉār.  mutal mutal avar pēccai eṉ kātu kēṭṭatu.  appoḻutu avariṭattil yāṉ kaṇṭatu nāṇam.  piṉṉē annīrmai avarai viṭuttup paṟantōṭiyatu.
1931ām āṇṭu jaṉavarit tiṅkaḷ mutal nāḷ katirēcac ceṭṭiyār, pālacuppiramaṇiya mutaliyār mutaliyōr tiruttaṇikai nōkkiṉar.  avaruṭaṉ yāṉuñ ceṉṟēṉ.  caravaṇap poykaik karaiyil mīṉāṭcicuntaram piḷḷaiyum, avartam āruyirttōḻar aṇṇāmalai mutaliyārum amarntiruttalaik kaṇṭōm.  avvēḷaiyil pālacuppira
maṇiya mutaliyār eṅkēyō pōyt tirumpi, cāppāṭu kōvintā eṉṟu kaivirittuk, kurukkaḷukkuk kaṭitam eḻutiṉēṉ.  avar ceṉṉai pōntaṉarām eṉṟār.  ‘foj¤
âš cWâbfh©L e©g®fis miH¤J tuyhkh? eṉṟu mutaliyārai yāṉ kēṭṭēṉ.  mJ nghœJ, ‘g£oÅ¡ fiyiaí« gÆyš nt©lhkh? eṉṟār mīṉāṭcicuntara
ṉār.  kalaikkuk kalai niṉaivu tōṉṟiyatu.
1938il kāṅkira āṭci naṭaipeṟṟa kālattil ceṉṉaic cūḷaiyil oru perum vēlai niṟuttam nikaḻntatu.  orunāḷ ōriṭattil pōli taṭiyaṭi naṭantateṉṟa ceyti mēṭaikku eṭṭiyatu.  ‘ekJ M£áÆyh joao? eṉṟu kāṅkira mantiri capaiyait tākkiṉēṉ;  uramākat tākkiṉēṉ.  kūṭṭamē kuluṅkiyatu.  arukirunta mīṉāṭcicuntara
ṉārkku eṉṉa tōṉṟiyatō teriyavillai.  avar eṉṉait toṭarntu vīṭṭukku vantār.  eṉṉai viṭṭup pirintu cella avar maṉam eḻavillai;  tikaittu tikaittu niṉṟār.  eṉṉa kāraṇam?
1940ām āṇṭil cattiyākkirakattil īṭupaṭṭu mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār ciṟaikkōṭṭam naṇṇiṉār.  ataic ciṟaikkalai payilum vāyppum cuntaraṉārkkuk kiṭaittatu pōlum eṉṟu yāṉ karutalāṉēṉ.
1941ām āṇṭu tiruppōrūril citampara cuvāmikaḷ kurupūcai naṭaipeṟṟatu.  akkurupūcaikkup palar kuṭumpat
tuṭaṉ pōtaruvatu vaḻakkam.  mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉārum pōntaṉar.  orupōtu avar tam āṇ kuḻavi vīriṭṭatu.  mīṉāṭcicuntara
ṉār appā eṉṟu ōṭiṉār.  avvōṭṭam avarait tiru
vaḷḷuvarāka eṉakkup pulappaṭuttiyatu;  avartam uṇmai vētāntamum pulaṉākiyatu.  cila ēṭṭu vētāntikaḷ kuṭumpattiṉ mītum kuḻantaikaḷ mītum erintu viḻuvatai yāṉ pārttirukkiṟēṉ.
1943ām āṇṭu ṭicampar 27, 28, 29ām nāṭkaḷil maturaiyil caiva cittānta āṇṭu viḻā eṉ talaimaiyil naṭaipeṟṟatu.  coṟpoḻivāḷaruḷ mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉārum oruvar.  avarukku camājattār koṭutta poruḷ camayamum puraṭciyum eṉpatu.  mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār puraṭcik kutirai evvaḻiyil ōṭumō eṉṟu cila iṭaṅkaḷil karutappaṭṭatu,  kalai eḻuntatu  ettaṉaiyō āyiram āyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar oru ciṟpaṉ iruntāṉ.  avaṉ oru cilaiyilē mīṉāṭci ōviyam eṭuttāṉ.  avaṉ evaṉō?  eppoḻutō maṟaintāṉ?  avaṉ eṭutta ōviyam paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷāka ilaṭcak kaṇakkāṉa makkaḷāl pōṟṟappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.  oruvaṉ kallai ōviyamākkit teyvamākkiyatu puraṭci
yaṉṟō?... eṉṟu muḻaṅkit tōṟṟuvāy ceytatu.  kūṭṭam imaiyā nāṭṭam uṭaiyatāyiṟṟu.
camāja viḻā muṭintatum yāṉ kāraikkuṭi hintu matāpimāṉa caṅkattiṉ veḷḷi viḻāvil talaimai vakikkac ceṉṟēṉ.  aṅkē mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār tamiḻ viruntu (01.01.1944) naṭantatu.  atilē, yāṉ uttarakōcamaṅkaikkuc ceṉṟu tirumpiṉēṉ.  aṅkē campantar āṭalpurintu pāṭun tiruk
kōla ōviyaṅ kaṇṭēṉ eṉṟār.  campantar āṭal ōviyam kalai uḷḷattil paṭivatu iyaṟkai eṉṟu yāṉ muṭivu kūṟiṉēṉ.
āṇṭutōṟum mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār oru tamiḻk kalaimānāṭu kūṭṭuvar.  atil avar eṉ talaimaiyai maṟappa
tillai.  avar talaimaiyil naṭaipeṟṟu varuñ ceṉṉai vētānta capaiyiṉ āṇṭu viḻāvum eṉatu talaimaiyai viḻaivatuṇṭu.
kalaiyulakil mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār vāḻkkaikkum eṉ vāḻkkaikkum uṟṟa toṭarpuk kuṟippukkaḷ cila muṉṉaruñ collappaṭṭaṉa.
mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār piṟavi puṇṇiyamuṭaiyatu.  ēṉ?  avar vāḻum ulakil ikalillai;  pakaiyillai.  aḵtu antaṇa ulakam.  aḵtu eṉakkup pulaṉākiṟatu.  orumai iṉpam!
maṟu patippil
eṉ vāḻkkaiyuṭaṉ toṭarpukoṇṭa aṉpar iṉṉuñ cilar uḷar.  avar tam aṉpu maṟupatippil vaḻiyum.
kuṟippu
vāḻkkaikkuriya vīṭukaḷ pala uṇṭu.  avaikaḷuḷ uṭal oru vīṭu; kuṭumpam oru vīṭu; ūr oru vīṭu;  nāṭu oru vīṭu;  ulakam oru vīṭu; ivaikaḷuḷ ciṟantatu uṭalākiya vīṭu.  itu caktipīṭam pōṉṟatu.  uṭal vīṭu cemmaiyāyiruntāl maṟṟa vīṭukaḷiṉ nalattukkuc cēvai ceytal kūṭum.  itupaṟṟiyē ulakil tōṉṟiya periyōr palarum uṭalaik kōyil eṉṟu orumukamāka aruḷip pōntaṉar.
ittuṇaic ciṟappu vāynta uṭalākiya vīṭṭai yāṉ iḷamaiyil pēṇiyē vantēṉ;  piṉṉē eṉṉaic cūḻnta toṇṭukaḷ ataip pēṇun toṇṭukkup pōtiya ōyvu taravillai.  pēccut toṇṭu eṉatu uṭal nilaiyaip perituṅ kulaittatu.
pēccut toṇṭu vēṇṭaṟpālatē.  aḵtu iyaṟkai varampaik kaṭantu cellutal kūṭātu.  eṉatu pēccu iyaṟkai varampaik kaṭantu ceṉṟatu.  ataṉāl uṭal vaḷaṉ kulaintatu.  iḵtu aṟamākumā;  annilaimai nērntatu!  yāṉ eṉ ceyvēṉ!
yāṉ uṭalōmpalaik kuṟittu ettaṉai nūlkaḷ payiṉṟēṉ!  ettaṉaip pēccukkaḷ pēciṉēṉ!  ettaṉai eḻuttukkaḷ eḻutiṉēṉ!  eṉṉaip piṟarkkōtum pētai
yaṉ eṉṉalām.  pēccāl uṭal nalattaik keṭuttuk
koṇṭamaikku eṉatu vāḻkkai ōr ilakkiyamāy niṟkiṟatu.  iḵtu iḷaiñarkku eccarikkai ceyyumeṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ.
inta ālayattai iṭittup pōṭuṅkaḷ;  mūṉṟu nāḷil atai eḻuppiviṭuvēṉ eṉṟār.  appoḻutu yūtar:  inta ālayattaik kaṭṭa nāṟpattāṟu varuṣam ceṉṟatē.  nīr itai mūṉṟu nāḷil eḻuppiviṭuvīrō eṉṟārkaḷ.  avarō tamatu carīramākiya ālayattaik kuṟittup pēciṉār.
- yōvāṉ 3 : 19 - 22
kāyamē kōyi lākak kaṭimaṉam aṭimai yāka
vāymaiyē tūymai yāka maṉamaṇi iliṅka māka
nēyamē neyyum pālā niṟaiyanīr amaiya āṭṭip 
pūcaṉai īca ṉārkkup pōṟṟavik kāṭṭi ṉōmē.

uḷḷam peruṅkōyil ūṉuṭam pālayam
vaḷḷaṟ pirāṉārkku vāykō puravācal
teḷḷat teḷintārkkuc cīvaṉ civaliṅkam
kaḷḷap pulaṉaintuṅ kāḷā maṇiviḷakkē.
uṭampār aḻiyiṉ uyirār aḻivar
tiṭampaṭa meyññāṉam cēravu māṭṭār
uṭampai vaḷarkkum upāya maṟintē
uṭampai vaḷarttēṉ uyirvaḷart tēṉē. 
uṭampiṉai muṉṉam iḻukkeṉ ṟiruntēṉ
uṭampiṉuk kuḷḷē uṟuporuḷ kaṇṭēṉ
uṭampuḷē uttamaṉ kōyilkoṇ ṭāṉeṉṟu

- tirumūlar

16. iṟuvāy
kaṇṇutalāṉ taṉkaruṇaik kaṇkāṭṭa vanteyti 
eṇṇutaṟ keṭṭā eḻilār kaḻal iṟaiñci
viṇṇiṟaintu maṇṇiṟaintu mikkāy viḷaṅkoḷiyāy
eṇṇiṟantu ellai ilātāṉē niṉperuñcīr 
pollā viṉaiyēṉ pukaḻumā ṟoṉṟaṟiyēṉ
pullākip pūṭāyp puḻuvāy maramākip
palviruka mākip paṟavaiyāyp pāmpākik
kallāy maṉitarāyp pēyāyk kaṇaṅkaḷāy
vallacura rāki muṉivarāyt tēvarāyc
cellāa niṉṟavit tāvara caṅkamattuḷ
ellāp piṟappum piṟantiḷaittēṉ.
				-  tiruvācakam
tiru. vē. viruttācala marattiṉiṉṟum oru vitai ciṉṉammāḷ nilattil viḻuntatu.  atu muḷaittuc ceṭiyāki maramāki niṟkiṟatu.  aḵtu aṟupatāṇṭaik kaṭantatu.  ammarattiṉ aṟupatāṇṭu varalāṟṟaik kūṟuvatu innūl.
aṭaivu 
eṉ vayatu eṉṉa?  aṟupattoṉṟā naṭakkiṟatu?  antō eṉ uyir eppoḻutu tōṉṟiyatu?  atu mutal mutal eppoḻutu uṭal peṟṟatu?  niṉaintu niṉaintu pārkkiṟēṉ.  āḻa niṉaintu niṉaintu pārkkiṟēṉ.  eṉakku oṉṟum eṭṭavillai.
muṟpiṟappu uṇṭā?  uṇṭu.  atil eṉakku aiyappāṭu illai; ciṟitum illai.  mutal tōṟṟan teriyavillai.  āṉāl yāṉ ettaṉaiyō piṟavikaḷ eṭuttuḷḷēṉ eṉpatu terikiṟatu.  yāṉ mara uṭalaṅkaḷ tāṅkiyiruppēṉ;  mīṉ uṭalaṅkaḷ tāṅkiyiruppēṉ;  puḷ uṭalaṅkaḷ ēṟṟiruppēṉ; vilaṅku uṭalaṅkaḷ ēṟṟiruppēṉ.  maṉita uṭal mutal mutal eṉakku eññāṉṟu vāyttatō aṟikilēṉ.  ippoḻutu ippiṟavi eytiyirukkiṟēṉ.  ippiṟavi eṉṉa aṭaintatu?
vāḻkkaiyiṉ aṭaivu poruṭcelvap pēṟu eṉṟu cilaviṭaṅkaḷil karutappaṭukiṟatu.  eṉakku appaṭit tōṉṟa
villai.  ariya vāḻkkaiyiṉ aṭaivu tōṉṟi niṉṟu aḻiyum veṟum poruṭ celvattaiyā poṟuttu niṟkum?  itupaṟṟi yāṉ tōṟṟuvāyiluñ cila urai vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷēṉ.
poruṭ celvam
em vāḻkkai poruṭ celvam peṟṟatā?  eṉatu vāḻkkai nūl eṉṉa colkiṟatu?
yāṉ piṟantu vaḷara vaḷara eṉ tantaiyār vaittirunta cila nilapulaṅkaḷum, īṭṭiya ciṟu poruḷum paṭippaṭiyē karaintaṉa.  eṉ vāḻkkaiyātal poruḷ tiraṭṭa iṭam peṟṟatā?  illai. yāṉ peṉcar kampeṉiyai viṭātiruntāl piṉṉāḷil peruñ campaḷam peṟuvōṉākiyiruppēṉ;  veli kallūriyiṉiṉṟum vilakātiruntāl pala tiṟap pāṭaputtakaṅkaḷ eḻutiyiruppēṉ;  poruṭ celvam kuvintirukkum.  poruṭ celvar cilar eṉakkut tuṇaipuriya vantapōtum, atai ēṟka eṉ maṉam icaiyavillai.  eṉatu uṭal nalaṉukkeṉṟu ciṟu ciṟu utavikaḷ kiṭaikkum.  avaikaḷum eṉakkup peritum payaṉpaṭṭatillai.  akkataikaḷai virittuk kūṟal anāvaciyam.  muṉṉē cila kūṟiṉēṉ.  iṅkē iraṇṭoṉṟu kuṟittal cālum.
parmā, malēyā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟun tāynāṭu tirumpuñ cila nēyarkaḷ eṉakku nalla nalla paṭukkaikaḷ, talaiyaṇaikaḷ, kampukaḷ, viciṟikaḷ mutaliyaṉa koṇṭu varuvārkaḷ.  avaikaṭkeṉṟu eppaṭiyō cilar pōtaruvar.  oruvar aṉpu, mika aḻakiya velveṭṭup paṭukkaiyāy  vantatu.  atu tāṉ vanta vārattilēyē oru pulavarai eḷiyēṉ vīṭṭukku aḻaittatu;  avaruḷ pukuntatu.  pulavar maṉam vel
veṭṭup paṭukkaimītu.  naṭantatai yāṉ uṇarntēṉ.  aḵtu avarkkuk koṭukkappaṭṭatu.  iṉṉoruvar aṉpu ōr aḻakiya viciṟiyāyt tiraṇṭatu.  atu mūṉṟu nāḷ eṉ vayattiliruntatu;  nāṉkāmnāḷē eṉṉaik kāṇa vanta oru canniyāciyiṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟatu.  tiruvaṇṇāmalaiyilē ōriravu oruvar pāl koṇarntaṉar.  cila tirāṭcaiyuṇṭu pāl aruntalām eṉṟiruntēṉ.  aṅkē annērattil oruvar paciyuṭaṉ vantār.  pālum paḻamum avarukku uṇavāyiṉa.  eṉakkeṉṟu vātumai paḻam mutaliyaṉa vīṭu cērum.  avaiviruntiṉarkkō maṟṟavarkkō payaṉpaṭum.  itaṟku viḷakkam yāraik kēṭṭal vēṇṭum?  iyaṟkai iṟaiyai
yaṉṟō kēṭṭal vēṇṭum?
maṉaivi makkaḷ oru peruñcelvam.  accelvamātal eṉ vāḻkkaiyil nilaittatā?  illaiyē.  yāṉ conta nilaiyamum illātavaṉ.
toṇṭuc celvam
vāḻkkaikku ētēṉum oru celvam tēvai.  eccelvamum peṟāta vāḻkkai vāḻkkaiyākātu.  eṉ vāḻkkai ētēṉuñ celvam peṟṟatā?  eṉ vāḻkkai oruvakaic celvam peṟṟatu.  atu toṇṭuc celvam.
eṉmāṭṭup poruṭ celvam kuvintiruntāl, eṉ vāḻkkai toṇṭuc celvattai aṭaintirukkumō eṉṉavō teriyavillai.  eṉpāl toṇṭuc celvam perukumāṟum, poruṭ celvam perukātavāṟum aruḷ curanta iyaṟkai iṟaiyai vāḻttal vēṇṭāmā?  atu vāḻka!
eṉ vāḻkkai paltiṟat toṇṭukaḷālākiyatu.  atu toṇṭup poṅkalaiyaṉṟi vēṟoṉṟai aṟiyumō?  eṉ vāḻkkaiyai nōkkuṅkaḷ;  kēḷuṅkaḷ.  eṉ uḷḷam poṅkutaṟkuriya vēṟoṉṟaip peṟṟirukkiṟatā?  ārāyntu pāruṅkaḷ.
poruṭ perukkai ulakil tōṉṟiya cāṉṟōr palarum orumukamākak kaṭintu ceṉṟaṉar.  kāraṇam eṉṉa kūṟalām?  poruṭ perukku, toṇṭaip perukkaviṭātu eṉpatu cāṉṟōr karuttuppōlum.  cāṉṟar moḻikaḷil āḻntu camātiyāṉāl pala nuṭpaṅkaḷ viḷaṅkum.
toṇṭil vēṭkai eḻuntu, atu ceyal āka ākap poruṭ perukkuk kavalai tāṉē paṭippaṭiyē poṉṟum.  poruṭ kavalaiyaip pōkkavallatu toṇṭē.  itai anupavattil uṇaralām.
kaḷḷac ceyal
poruḷiṉṟi ulakil eppaṭi vāḻtal kūṭum?  iḵtoru kēḷvi;  peruṅ kēḷvi.  vāḻkkaikkup poruḷ tēvaiyē;  vēṇṭāmeṉṟu evaruṅ kūṟār.  tēvaikku mēṟpaṭṭa poruḷ ēṟṟukku?  atu kūṭāteṉpatu cāṉṟōr koḷkai atu kaḷḷacceyal eṉpatum avartam aṟivuṟuttal.
toṇṭaṉum maṟṟavaṉum
toṇṭaṉ taṉatu tēvaikkuriya poruḷaippaṟṟiyuṅ kavalaiyuṟuvatillai.  maṟṟavaṉ kavalaiyuṟukiṟāṉ.  iṅkētāṉ toṇṭaṉukkum maṟṟavaṉukkum vēṟṟumai eḻukiṟatu.  toṇṭaṉ kaṭavuḷuṇmaiyil uṟutiyuṭaiyavaṉ;  ellā
vaṟṟaiyum ceytavaṉ:  taṉakkeṉṟu vāḻātavaṉ.  yāṉ eppaṭi vāḻvēṉ eṉṉuṅ kavalai avaṉukkut tōṉṟuvatillai.  avaṉaik kākkum poṟuppuk kaṭavuḷuṭaiyatākiṟatu.  maṟṟavaṉ kaṭavuḷuṇmaiyil uṟutiyillātavaṉ;  aiyamuṭaiyavaṉ.  vāḻkkaikku eṉ ceyvatu eṉṉum naṭukkam avaṉaip pīṭikkiṟatu.  naṭukkam avaṉai ācaip pēykku iraiyākku
kiṟatu.
etai uṇpōm etaik kuṭippōmeṉṟu uṅkaḷ uyirukkākavum, etai uṭuppōmeṉṟu uṅkaḷ uṭalukkākavum kavalaippaṭātiruṅkaḷeṉṟu uṅkaḷukkuc colkiṟēṉ.  uṇavai
viṭa uyirum uṭaiyai viṭa uṭalum mukkiyamallavā?  ākāyattup paṭcikaḷaik kavaṉittup pāruṅkaḷ;  avaikaḷ vitaikkiṟatu
millai aṟukkiṟatumillai;  kaḷañciyaṅkaḷil cērttuvaikkiṟatu
millai;  avaikaḷaiyum uṅkaḷ paramapitā piḻaippūṭṭukiṟār.  avaikaḷaip pārkkilum nīṅkaḷ vicēṣittavarkaḷallavā?  kavalaippaṭukiṟatiṉālē uṅkaḷil evaṉ taṉ āyuḷiṉ aḷavōṭu oru muḻattaik kūṭṭuvāṉ?  uṭaikkākavum nīṅkaḷ kavalaip
paṭukiṟateṉṉa?  kāṭṭup puṣpaṅkaḷ eppaṭi vaḷarukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu kavaṉittup pāruṅkaḷ;  avaikaḷ uḻaikkiṟatumillai nūṟkiṟatu
millai;  eṉṟālum cālomōṉē taṉ carva makimaiyilum ivaikaḷil oṉṟaippōl uṭuttiyiruntatillai  yeṉṟu uṅkaḷukkuc collukiṟēṉ.  iṉṟaikkiruntu nāḷaikku aṭuppilē pōṭappaṭum kāṭṭup pullaik kaṭavuḷ ivvitamāy uṭuttiṉārāṉāl aṟpa vicuvācikaḷē, uṅkaḷai uṭuttuvatu atika niccayamallavā?  ātalāl, etai uṇpōm etaik kuṭippōm etai uṭuppōmeṉṟu kavalaippaṭavēṇṭām.  ivaṟṟai
yellām puṟajātiyār nāṭit tēṭukiṟārkaḷ.  ivaiyellām uṅkaḷukkut tēvaiyeṉṟu uṅkaḷ paramapitā aṟivār.  mutalāvatu kaṭavuḷuṭaiya rājyattaiyum avaruṭaiya nītiyaiyun tēṭuṅkaḷ.  appoḻutu ivaiyellām uṅkaḷukkukkūṭak koṭukkappaṭum.  ākaiyāl, nāḷaiya tiṉattiṟkākak kavalaip
paṭātiruṅkaḷ.
				        - mattēyu 6:25-34
ic cemmoḻikaḷai yāṉ piḷḷaimaiyil payiṉṟēṉ;  paricil nōkkuṭaṉ payiṉṟēṉ.  avai eṉṉai viṭṭakala
villai;  eṉṉuḷ niṉṟu niṉṟu eṉṉaip paṇpaṭuttiyē vantaṉa.
ikal - pakai
iḷamaiyil vilaṅkuṇarvu eṉṉai āṭṭiyatu.  karuttu vēṟṟumaiyuṭaiyār mītu pāmpāyc cīṟuvēṉ;  puliyāyp pāyvēṉ;  collukkuc collum,  kallukku  kallum, mallukku mallum viṭuvēṉ.  piṉṉē nilaimai māṟiyatu.  pāmpum puliyum eṅkēyō ōṭiṉa.  eṉṉai vaivōr mītum eṉakku muṉivu tōṉṟuvatillai;  kaṉivē tōṉṟukiṟatu.
toṇṭu perukap peruka vilaṅkuṇarvu tāṉē vilakum.  vilaṅku uṇarvu vilaka vilaka ikal pakai eḻucci peṟā
toṭuṅkum.  ivvoṭukkattukku eṉatu vāḻkkai oru cāṉṟu.  itaṟku eṉatu kalvi, kēḷvi, nalliṇakkam, ilvāḻkkai, 
yāṉ īṭupaṭṭa iyakkaṅkaḷ tuṇai ceytaṉa.  ivaikaḷuṭaṉ āṉṟōr moḻikaḷ eṉṉuḷ niṉṟu aṉput toṇṭai vaḷarttaṉa.  ammoḻikaḷ cila varumāṟu:-
kaṇṇukkuk kaṇ, pallukkup pal eṉṟu uraittatākak kēṭṭirukkiṟīrkaḷ.  nāṉō uṅkaḷukkuc collukiṟēṉ;  tīmaiyōṭu etirttu niṟkavēṇṭām:  uṉṉai valatu kaṉṉattil aṟaikiṟavaṉukku maṟukaṉṉattaiyum tiruppik kāṭṭu.  uṉṉōṭu vaḻakkāṭi uṉ uḷvatirattai eṭuttuk koḷḷa virumpu
kiṟavaṉukku uṉ mēl vatirattaiyum viṭṭuviṭu.  oruvaṉ uṉṉai oru mail tūram varak kaṭṭāyañ ceytāl avaṉōṭu iraṇṭu mail tūram pō.  uṉṉiṭam kēṭkiṟavaṉukkuk koṭu.  uṉṉiṭam kaṭaṉ vāṅka virumpu kiṟavaṉukku mukaṅ kōṇātē.
uṉ ayalāṉil aṉpukūru, uṉ catturuvaiyō pakai eṉṟu collappaṭṭatākak kēṭṭirukkiṟīrkaḷ;  nāṉ uṅkaḷukkuc colvatō:  uṅkaḷ catturukkaḷil aṉpu  kūruṅkaḷ;  uṅkaḷai imcaippaṭuttukiṟavarkaḷukkāka jepañ ceyyuṅkaḷ.  ippaṭic ceytāl paralōkattilirukkiṟa uṅkaḷ pitāvukkup piḷḷai
kaḷāyiruppīrkaḷ;  avar tīyōrmēlum nallōrmēlum tamatu cūriyaṉai utikkacceytu nīti yuḷḷavarkaḷ mēlum nīti
yillātavarkaḷ mēlum maḻai peyyac ceykiṟār.  uṅkaḷaic cinēkikkiṟavarkaḷaiyē nīṅkaḷum cinēkippīrkaḷāṉāl uṅka
ḷukkup palaṉ eṉṉa?  āyakkārarum appaṭiyē ceykiṟār
kaḷallavā?  uṅkaḷ cakōtararai māttiram vāḻttuvīrkaḷāṉāl nīṅkaḷ vicēṣamāyc ceykiṟateṉṉa?  puṟajātiyārum appaṭic ceykiṟārkaḷallavā?  ākaiyāl paralōkattilirukkiṟa uṅkaḷ pitā pūraṇa caṟkuṇarā yirukkiṟatupōla nīṅkaḷum pūraṇa caṟkuṇarā yiruṅkaḷ.				   - mattēyu 5 : 38 -48
immeymmoḻikaḷ teyva olikaḷ.  ivaikaṭku yāṉ cevi cāyttavaṇṇamirukkiṟēṉ.
eṉṉaip poṟutta aḷavil yāṉ evariṭattilum pakaimai pārāṭṭuvatillai.  piṟar eṉṉiṭam pakaimai pārāṭṭāmalillai.
cilar poruḷutavi nāṭi eṉṉiṭam varuvar.  iyaṉṟa utavi puriyumāṟu tamaiyaṉāriṭañ colvēṉ.  iraṇṭoruvar avvutavi peṟṟuc celvar;  maṟṟavar tām etirpārtta tokai vēṇṭumeṉṟu valiyuṟuttuvar.  peruntokaikku yāṉ eṅkē pōvēṉ?  kūṭṭaṅkaḷilum maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum avar vāy eṉṉai vaiyum.  yāṉ vēlaic cūḻaliṭai neruk
kuṇṭu kiṭakkuṅkāl cilar kaṭṭurai kēṭpar vēlaiccūḻal; maṉṉikka eṉpēṉ.  camayam nērumpōtu avaratu eḻutukōl eṉṉaip puṭaikkum.  kāraṇamiṉṟip poṟāmai pūkkum neñciṉaraiyum yāṉ kaṇṭirukkiṟēṉ.  karuttu vēṟṟumai kāraṇamākavum, kaṭcip piṇakkuk kāraṇamākavum ikal koṇṭu eṉṉait tākkutalil maṉañ celuttuvōrumuḷar.  iṉṉōraṉṉavar uḷḷattilum eṉṉaip poṟuttup pakaimai mūṭṭāta vāḻkkai eṉakkuk kiṭaikkumā?  itu pērācaiyākumā?  evariṭattum pakaimai pārāṭṭāta an taṇmaiyai eṉpāl paṭivittavaṉ iṟaivaṉ.  eṉṉaip poṟuttap piṟar uḷḷattum, pakaimai eḻāta nilaiyoṉ
ṟirukkumāyiṉ, atai eṉakku vaḻaṅkumāṟu avviṟaivaṉai vēṇṭukiṟēṉ.  cōkrattaraiyum, kiṟituvaiyum, meyp
poruḷ nāyaṉāraiyum ulakam koṉṟatu eṉ niṉaivil uṟāmal illai.
tīmaiyum antaṇmaiyum
uyir cārntataṉ vaṇṇamākum iyalpuṭaiyatu.  ataṉāl tīyōr kūṭṭuṟavu vēṇṭām eṉṟu āṉṟōr palarum aruḷic ceṉṟaṉar.  eṉ vāḻkkai peṟṟa nallōr iṇakkattai yāṉ āṅkāṅkē kuṟittu vantuḷḷēṉ.  toṇṭu yārukkut tēvai?  nallōrkkā?  maṟṟavarkkā?  itu kuṟittuk kiṟituperumāṉ tirumoḻi muṉṉar eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
tīyōr kūṭṭuṟavu kūṭātu eṉṟu āṉṟōr ēṉ aruḷiṉar?  āṉṟōr moḻi toṇṭarai uḷaṅkoṇṭu eḻuntataṉṟu;  maṟṟavarai uḷaṅkoṇṭu eḻuntatu.
nallōriṭait toṇṭu nikaḻavēṇṭuvatillai.  avarkkut toṇṭu eṟṟukku?  tīyōriṭai nikaḻvatē toṇṭākum.  ivarkkut toṇṭu tēvai.  tīmai vayappaṭum uḷḷamuṭaiyār toṇṭarākār.  avar toṇṭil iṟaṅkutal tavaṟu.  toṇṭukkeṉṟu tammaip paṇpaṭuttik
koṇṭavarē toṇṭil iṟaṅkutal nallatu. ataṟku aṟikuṟi eṉṉa?  tamakkut tīṅku ceyvōriṭattuṅ kaṉaṉṟu pakaimai koḷḷāmai.  ituvē antaṇmai eṉpatu.
nallōriṇakkam nallōriṇakkam eṉṟu aṟai kūvi vanta yāṉ ippoḻutu evariṭattilum paḻakukiṟēṉ; cēvai ceykiṟēṉ.  tīmai eṉṉai oṉṟuñ ceyvatillai.  eṉṉiṭam pāmpillai;  puliyillai.  pāmpum puliyum iṉi eṉṉai eṉ ceyyum?  eṉatu antaṇmai ōraḷavil innilaiyai eyti
yuḷḷatu.
toṇṭiṉ aṭaivu eṉṉa?  antaṇmai.  eṉ vāḻkkai antaṇmaiyai aṭaintatā?  ōraḷavil aṭaintatu.  iṉṉum atu kaṉital vēṇṭum.  ataṟku iṉṉum toṇṭu ceytal vēṇṭumeṉṟu eṉakkut terikiṟatu.  toṇṭu kuṟaipāṭṭai nīkkum.
cōtaṉai
iḷamaiyil pukaḻ nāṭṭattil yāṉ mūḻkik kiṭantēṉ.  piṉṉē toṇṭil īṭupaṭappaṭa - antaṇmaiyai arumpa arumpa - pukaḻnāṭṭam eṉṉai aṟiyāmalē poṉṟivantatu.  annāṭṭam poṉṟiyatā?  illaiyā?  eṉpataṟku oru cōtaṉai nikaḻntatu.  atu maṇiviḻāk koṇṭāṭṭam.
maṇi viḻākkaḷ
ceṉṉaiyil tivāṉpakatūr teyvacikāmaṇi mutaliyār maṇiviḻā (1941) koṇṭāṭappaṭṭatu.  ak koṇṭāṭṭat
til eṉatu coṟpoḻivukaḷ mūṉṟu naṭantaṉa.  avaikaḷaik koṇṭatē uḷḷoḷi eṉṉum nūl.  avviḻāt talaivar teyvacikāmaṇi mutaliyār eṉṉaip pārttu, uṅkaḷ maṇiviḻā vēṟu vaḻiyil naṭakkum.  yāṉ tiṭṭaṅ kōliyuḷ
ḷēṉ eṉṟu colvar.  ‘ah‹ kidÉ k¡fŸ ïšyhjt‹;  vd¡F kÂÉHh v‰W¡F? eṉṟu maṟuppēṉ.  atu kuṟittu eṅkaḷukkuḷ vātam nikaḻum.
am maṇiviḻāvait toṭarntu eṉ tamaiyaṉār maṇiviḻā naṭaipeṟṟatu.  atu pōḻtu cilar, ivviḻā tiru.vi.ka. maṇiviḻāviṉ muṉṉaṇi eṉṟu vāḻttuk kūṟiṉar.  akkūṟṟuc ceṉṉaiyampatiyil ulaviyē vantatu.  eṉ tamaiyaṉār maṇiviḻā eṉatu aṟupatāṇṭu niṟaivai niṉai
vūṭṭutaṟku mūlamāka niṉṟatu.
ōrāṇṭu muṉṉarē
ōrāṇṭu muṉṉarē (1942) caktitā cuppiramaṇiyattiṉ viraivu navacaktiyil eṉatu maṇiviḻā varukaiyaik kuṟittu maṇiyōcai eḻuppiyatu.  accamayam navacaktiyil cuttāṉanta pāratiyār vāḻttu veḷivantatu.  yāṉ navacakti nilaiyam pōntu eṉṉai aṟiyāmal maṇiviḻāvaik kuṟippaṭṭataip
paṟṟi varuntukiṟēṉ;  iṉi atai valiyuṟuttātu viṭuka eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttiṉēṉ.  cuppiramaṇiyam eṉ vaṟpuṟuttalukkuc cevicāyttaṉar.
nāḷēṟa nāḷēṟa eṉakku iṉiyarum, tamiḻppulavarum tamiḻnāṭṭaip paḻaiyapaṭi nāṭākak kāṇṭal vēṇṭu
meṉṉum nōkkuṭaṉ toṇṭāṟṟupavarumākiya nāraṇa turaikkaṇṇaṉār uḷḷattilum, vēṟu cilar uḷḷattilum eṉatu aṟupatāṇṭu niṟaivu eṇṇam ūṟikkoṇṭiruntatu pōlum.  avaravar pattirikai muḻaṅkat toṭaṅkiyatu.  ammuḻakkam eṉ maṇṭaiyaik kuṭaintatu.
taṭai
ceṉṉait tamiḻccaṅka amaiccarum, eṉṉāruyirt tōḻarumākiya mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār uḷḷiṭṭa cilar puṟap
paṭṭaṉar.  yāṉ paṟpala kāraṇaṅ kūṟit taṭukkalāṉēṉ.  eṉ ceyvarō eṉṟa kiliyum eṉakku ēṟpaṭṭatu.
kālañ ceṉṟa caṇmuka mutaliyār mutalāṇṭu viḻāvai muṉṉiṭṭu (7-5-1943) orunilaiyan tiṟakka avar vāṇiyampāṭi nōkkiṉēṉ.  aṅkē kūṭṭattil talaimai vakitta maṇṭi kanta
cāmi mutaliyār, uṅkaḷ maṇiviḻā ciṟappākak koṇṭāṭap
paṭum pōlum;  ataṟkeṉṟu oru kuḻu nāḷaiyiṉaṉṟu amaiyumeṉṟu terikiṟatu eṉṟu eṉṉiṭaṅ kūṟiṉar.  appaṭi oṉṟum irātē eṉṟu yāṉ coṉṉēṉ.  avar tamakku vanta aṟikkaiyaik kāṭṭiṉar.  aṟikkaiyil mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār kaiccāttuk kaṇṭēṉ;  nammai aṟiyāmal eṉṉeṉṉavō naṭakkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu ēkkuṟṟēṉ.
kuḻu amaippu
ceṉṉai cērntēṉ.  mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār eṉṉaik
kaṇṭu kuḻu amaintuviṭṭatu.  talaivar:  si.e.irattiṉa
capāpati mutaliyār.  utavit talaivarkaḷ:  rājā. sar. aṇṇāmalai ceṭṭiyār, cakkaravartti irājakōpālāc
cāriyār, ī.ve. irāmacāmi nāyakkar, em.si. rājā. kē. pāla cuppiramaṇiya aiyar, tivāṉpakatūr ṭi.em. nārāyaṇacāmi piḷḷai mutaliyōr.  si.em. irāmaccantirañ ceṭṭiyārum yāṉuñ ceyalāḷar;  viḻāvai nāṅkaḷ koṇṭāṭik
koḷkiṟōm; atai muṟpaṭutti oruvitat tamiḻ uṇarcciyai nāṭṭil uṇṭākka virumpukiṟōm.  itaṟku maṟuppu vaḻaṅkuvatu naṉṟāyirātu eṉṟu āṟutal kūṟiṉār.  eṉakku oṉṟun tōṉṟavillai.  viḻā tamiḻ uṇarcci aḷavil nillātu, yāṉ virumpāta vēṟu vēṟu vaḻikaḷiluñ cellum eṉṟu colli, ekkāraṇam paṟṟiyum paṇamuṭippu vaḻaṅkutal kūṭātu;  ivvuṟutiyil nilluṅkaḷ eṉṟēṉ.  paṇamuṭippu vaḻaṅkac cilar virumpukiṟār;  yāṉum virumpukiṟēṉ.  atu kūṭāteṉṟu nīṅkaḷ maṟuppatāl atai viṭukiṟōm.  viḻāc celavukkeṉṟu ciṟu tokai taṇṭik koḷkiṟōm eṉṟa uṟuti veḷivantatu.  piṉṉē ceyalāḷar viṭutta viṇṇap
pattil paṇa muṭippuk kuṟippu viṭappaṭṭatu.  eṉ kavalai oḻintatu.  viṇṇappam tamiḻnāṭṭai eḻuppiyatu.
paṭṭiṉattār
nām paṟpala tuṟaikaḷil uḻaittuḷḷōm.  avvat
tuṟaiyiṉar maṇiviḻāk koṇṭāṭap puṟappaṭuvar.  koṇ
ṭāṭṭam pērāvaṇamākum.  avaravar vāḻttil pukaḻuraikaḷ potuḷum.  mitfŸ ekJ kndhÃiyia¡ Fiy¤jhš v‹ brŒtJ? eṉṟa cintaṉai eḻuntatu.  eṉ cintaṉai paṭṭiṉattār mītu naṭantatu.  paṭṭiṉattārai yāṉ viṭavillai.  avarai ōtiya vaṇṇamiruntēṉ;  avar tam aṟavuraikaḷ eṉṉaik kākkum eṉṟu nampiṉēṉ.
tuvakkam
maṇiviḻā tiruppōrūril tuvaṅkappaṭṭatu.  kuṭumpat toṭarpāka evvitak kiriyaiyum ceytal vēṇṭām eṉṟu tamaiyaṉārkku aṟivittēṉ.  avar ataṟku iṇaṅkiṉar.
pēraṉ iḷampūraṇaṉ pirārttaṉaiyai yoṭṭi nāṅkaḷ tiruppōrūr cellutal nērntatu.  aṅkē caiva cittānta makā camājam (23, 24.8.1943) maṇiviḻāvukkuk kālkoṇṭatu.  maṭātipati citampara caṇmuka cuvāmikaḷ ōmak kiriyai nikaḻtta muyaṉṟār.  atuvēṇṭāmeṉṟu kēṭṭuk koṇṭēṉ.  cuvāmikaḷ atai viṭuttuc cakara nāmārc caṉaiyil talaippaṭṭaṉar.  atai yāṉ taṭuttēṉillai.
nāṭeṅkum
maṇiviḻāk kaḻakattārāl ceṉṉaic cintātirip
pēṭṭai uyarnilaip paḷḷiyil 25ām nāḷ mutal aintu nāḷ viḻāk koṇṭāṭappaṭṭatu.  viḻā, kaḻaka aḷavil niṟka
villai.  ataik kaṭantu peruki veḷḷamākiyatu.  viḻāvaik koṇṭāṭat toḻiṟ caṅkaṅkaḷ puṟappaṭṭaṉa;  mātar caṅkaṅkaḷ puṟappaṭṭaṉa;  camayac capaikaḷ viraintaṉa; kalvik kaḻakaṅkaḷ viraintaṉa;  paḷḷikaḷ vīṟiṉa;  kallūrikaḷ vīṟiṉa;  pattirikaikaḷ paṟantaṉa;  nūlakaṅkaḷ nuḻaintaṉa;  ciṟaikaḷ eḻuntaṉa;  nakaracapaikaḷ kiḷampiṉa;  mūlai muṭukkukaḷum muyaṉṟaṉa.  kī. irāmaliṅkam ciṟukarumpūril eṉṉait tamiḻ nāṭu eṉṟatu tīrkka taricaṉamāyiṟṟu.  viḻāt toṭaṅki āṟu tiṅkaḷāyiṉa.  iṉṉum koṇṭāṭṭam niṟkavillai.
koṇṭāṭṭa nikaḻccikaḷai maṇiviḻāk kaḻakam oru malarāka veḷiyiṭa muyaṉṟu varukiṟateṉṟu aṟikiṟēṉ.  vivaraṅkaḷai ammalaril pārkka.  mīṉāṭcuntaraṉār karutiya
vāṟu tamiḻ uṇarcci nāṭeṅkum tatumpiyatu.
yāṉ etirpārttapaṭi vāḻttukkaḷilum piṟavaṟṟilum pukaḻuraikaḷ poṅkip poṅki vaḻintaṉa.  paṭṭiṉattār pāṭalō vēṟu etuvō pukaḻuraikaṭku eṉṉai iraiyāk
kāmal kāttatu.  cōtaṉai eṉṉait taṉvayappaṭuttavillai.
piṉṉar
cupāṉu āṇṭu āvaṇittiṅkaḷ mirukacīriṭa nāḷuṭaṉ (24.8.1943) eṉatu aṟupatāvatu akavai niṟaiveytiyatu.  piṉṉar eṉṉeṉṉavō nikaḻntaṉa.  iraṇṭoṉṟaic ciṟappākac collalāmeṉṟu karutukiṟēṉ.
camāja viḻā
maturaiyil caiva cittānta makā camāja āṇṭu viḻā (27, 28, 29-12-1943) eṉ talaimaiyil naṭaipeṟṟatu.  varavēṟput talaivar :  tiruñāṉacampantacuvāmikaḷ maṭattut talaivar cuvāmināta tēcikar;  ceyalāḷar:  aṅkayaṟkaṇṇi ālavāyk kōyil aṟattalaivar ār. e. nāyuṭu.
eṅkum ār.e.
ār. e. nāyuṭu oruvar. avarai yāṉ pukaivaṇṭi nilaiyattil kaṇṭēṉ;  kuḷikkumiṭattil kaṇṭēṉ;  kūṭṭattil kaṇṭēṉ;  cāppiṭumiṭattil kaṇṭēṉ;  eṅkum kaṇṭēṉ.  avarpāl veṟuṅ kaṭamaimaṭṭum nikaḻavillai.  avartam aṉpiṉ aṉpē corintatu.
tōḻamait toṇṭar
pukaivaṇṭi nilaiyattil ār.e. nāyuṭuvuṭaṉ vēṟu oruvaraiyum pārttēṉ.  avaraip pārttatum viṭutalai peṟṟatu pōṉṟatōr uṇarvu tōṉṟiyatu.  avar tūttukkuṭi la. nārāyaṇaṉ.
nārāyaṇaṉ tōḻamai eṉakku mutal mutal (1924) tūttukkuṭic caiva cittānta capaiyilē kiṭaittatu.  aṅkēyē tōḻamai tāyaṉput toṇṭākak kaṉintatu.  aṉṟutoṭṭu iṉṟuvarai yāṉ teṉṉāṭṭukkōṭi naṇṇumpō
tellām nārāyaṇaṉ eṉatu uṭal kāppāḷarāyt toṭarntu varutal oru viratamākiyatu.  vallanāṭu, kuṟṟālam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil niḻaleṉa eṉṉait toṭarntu avar āṟṟiya toṇṭukaḷellām niṉaivukku varukiṉṟaṉa.
nārāyaṇaṉ tēcapaktar;  ciṟaipukuntavar;  cīrtiruttak
kārar;  kalappumaṇak karuttar ;  ceyalar;  eṉatu uṭalnilai kuṟittuk kavalaiyuṟuvōruḷ oruvar.  avarukkōr arumai maṉaiviyār vāyttuḷḷaṉar.  avar āḻvārammaiyār.  eṉatu maṇiviḻāvil avar tam aṉpu karuppaṭṭi eṉṉum iṉimaip poruḷākac ceṉṉai cērntatu.
tōḻar nārāyaṇaṉ toṇṭupuriya maturaikkum pōntaṉar.  mūṉṟunāḷ eṉatu uṭalnalaṉ avarāl pala vaḻiyiluṅ kākkappaṭṭatu.  nārāyaṇaṉ nīṭu vāḻka.
ūrvalam
eṉ vāḻkkai ettaṉaiyōvita ūrvalaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭatu.  ūrvalattil eṉakkuc calippu.  maturaiyil ūr
valam irātu eṉṟu eṇṇiṉēṉ.  maturai ūrvalam periyatāyiṟṟu. 
tiruñāṉacampanta cuvāmikaḷ maṭattiṉiṉṟum ūrvalam puṟappaṭṭatu.  maṭattu mariyātaikaḷ eṉakkuc ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.  cīrtiruttac caivaṉukkum maṭattu mariyātai naṭakkiṟatē eṉṟu eṉ maṉam niṉaintatu.
eṉ maṉam
iraṭṭaik kutirai pūṭṭiya cittirap pū vaṇṭi; tirumuṟai tāṅkiya yāṉai oṉṟu;  vala iṭaṅkaḷil vēṟu yāṉaikaḷ;  oṭṭai kutiraikaḷ;  nakaruṅ koṭikaḷum tōraṇaṅkaḷum neṭuka; palatiṟak kuṭaik kuviyalkaḷ;  palliya muḻakkam; aṉpar īṭṭam; aṭiyavar kūṭṭam; maturait tiraṭci; makkaḷ makiḻcci.  eṉ maṉam tiruñāṉacampantaraiyum, maṅkaiyarkkaraciyāraiyum, kulacciṟaiyāraiyum eṇṇiyatu:  ālavāyp patikaṅkaḷai eṇṇiyatu;  tiruviḷaiyāṭalkaḷai eṇṇiyatu.
talaimaiyurai
ūrvalam tiruvītikaḷaic cuṟṟit tirukkōyilai aṭaintatu.  aṅkayaṟkaṇṇi paṅkaṉai maṉattāl toḻutu tirumaṇa maṇṭapattuḷ nuḻaintēṉ.  kūṭṭam - peruṅkūṭṭam - kaṇkoḷḷāk kāṭci.  varavēṟpu.  tiṟappu mutaliyaṉa nikaḻntaṉa.  talaimaiyurai eḻuttup pēccu.  cila ēṭukaḷaip paṭittup piṟavaṟṟai ōr iḷaiñaraik koṇṭu paṭikkac ceyyalām eṉṟu karutiyiruntēṉ.  pēccait toṭaṅkiyatum eṉakkup paḻaiya kural ōraḷavil eḻuntatu.  kaḷaippu uṇṭākavē illai.  muṟṟum talaimaiyuraiyai yāṉē paṭittēṉ.
muṭivurai
coṟpoḻivāḷar ceṟivu atikam.  ataṉāl mutal nāḷ karutturai curukkamāy muṭintatu.  iraṇṭām nāḷ kālaṅ kiṭaikkavē illai.  mūṉṟām nāḷ ēṟakkuṟaiya mūṉṟarai maṇi nēram karutturai kaḻaṟiṉēṉ.  karutturaikaḷil cila kuṟittal nalameṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu.
*** vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻip piṇakku nāṭṭil eḻumpi
yuḷḷatu.  appiṇakku ippoḻutu potumakkaḷiṭaip paravi
varukiṟatu.  iṅkē pēciya cilariṭattiruntu atu taṉṉai aṟiyāmalē veḷivantatu.  atupaṟṟiya kaṭitamoṉṟum eṉakku vantuḷḷatu.  ātaliṉ, eṉatu uḷḷakkiṭakkaiyaic ciṟitātal veḷiyiṭak kaṭamaippaṭṭirukkiṟēṉ.
vaṭakku moḻi vaṭa moḻi eṉṟum, teṟku moḻi teṉmoḻi eṉṟum poruḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  eṉakku tōṉṟum poruḷ vēṟu.  vaṭamoḻi ālamarattu moḻi eṉṟum, teṉmoḻi aḻakum iṉimaiyum poruntiya moḻi eṉṟum eṉakkut tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa.  vaṭam - ālamaram;  teṉ - aḻaku, iṉimai.  ālamarattaṭiyil uḷḷavar civapirāṉ.  vaṭaniḻaliluḷḷa civapirāṉ aruḷiyatu vaṭamoḻi.  vaṭamoḻi naṭarājaruṭaiya tamarukattiṉiṉṟum piṟantateṉṟu pāṇiṉi muṉivar kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.  teṉmoḻi civapirāṉiṭattiruntu piṟantateṉpatu marapākiyatu.  itupaṟṟi virivurai iṅkē vēṇṭuvatillai.  iraṇṭu moḻikkum piṟappiṭam oṉṟē.  ‘MÇaª jÄnHhoir ahdt‹ - TÇa Fz¤jh® F¿ Ã‹wt‹’ v‹W m¥gU«, ‘ïªâu‰F«*** brªâw¤j jÄnHhir tlbrhšyh»’ v‹W âUk§if MœthU« mUËÆU¤jY§ fUj‰ghyJ.***
tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ ellaiyaik kavaṉittal vēṇṭum.  tolkāppiyattukkuc ciṟappup pāyiraṅ kūṟappōnta paṉampāraṉār vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari āyiṭait tamiḻkūṟu nallulakattu eṉṟu aruḷiṉar.  iṉṉum pala nūṟṟāṇṭu muṉceṉṟu ellaiyai nōkkiṉāl tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ vaṭa ellai vintiyamāy viḷaṅkutal kāṇalām.  mēlum pala nūṟāṇṭu pōyp pārttāl avvellai imayamāyt tikaḻtal tōṉṟum.  tamiḻnāṭu imayamvarai irunta kāla
mumuṇṭu.  itai ippoḻutu kiṭaittuḷḷa mahōñ
catārō valiyuṟuttukiṟatu, ēṟakkuṟaiya ēḻāyiram āṇṭu
kaṭku muṉṉar intiyā muḻuvatum tamiḻnāṭāyp polinta
teṉṟu terikiṟatu.  annāḷil vaṭakkumoḻi etu?  teṟku moḻi etu?  upayavētāntam eṉṟōr āṭci namatu nāṭṭil vaḻakkiliruttalum uṉṉattakkatu.
vaṭamoḻiyum namatu nāṭṭu moḻi - teṉmoḻiyum namatu nāṭṭu moḻi eṉpatu eṉ karuttu.  ellāt tāy
mārkaṭkum oru nā;  nam tāykku iraṇṭu nā.
tamiḻar orē moḻi payilalvēṇṭum eṉṉum niyati illai;  pala moḻi payilalām;  tamiḻuṭaṉ vaṭamoḻi payilalām;  arāpi payilalām;  āṅkilam payilalām;  pirañcu payilalām;  lāttiṉ payilalām;  piṟavum payilalām;  pala
moḻip payiṟci tāymoḻikku ākkan tēṭuvatākum.  nacciṉārkkiṉiyar vaṭamoḻi payiṉṟa tamiḻar.  avar uraiyil tamiḻ maṇaṅ kamaḻtal kaṇkūṭu.  parimēlaḻakar vaṭamoḻiyil perum pulavar.  avar uraikkaṇ tamiḻttāy kūttāṭutal veḷḷiṭai malai.  civañāṉa muṉivar vaṭamoḻik kaṭalaiyum teṉmoḻik kaṭalaiyum uṇṭa peruṅ kāḷamēkam.  avar urainaṭaiyil tamiḻ aṉṉaiyiṉ tiruvōlakkam pulappaṭukiṟatu.  eṉ muṉṉē vīṟṟirukkum mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār palamoḻip pulavar.  avartam palamoḻip pulamai tamiḻukku evvaḷavu nalañ ceykiṟateṉpatu namakkut teriyum.  ātaliṉ, vaṭamoḻi payiṉṟāl tamiḻ māṇṭāpōkum?  tamiḻ āṟṟalillāta moḻiyā?  tamiḻ kaṉṉi āyiṟṟē.  vaṭamoḻi
yum payilalām; piṟa moḻiyum payilalām.  tāymoḻiyiṉ ceḻumaiyai maṭṭuṅ kāttal vēṇṭum.  tāymoḻi nalampēṇip pala moḻi payilvatai oru kaṭamaiyākavuṅ koḷḷalām.  ataṉāl tamiḻttāykkuk kēṭunērātu. nērumāyiṉ, tamiḻvīram vāḷā kiṭakkumō?  ākavē vaṭamoḻimītu kāḻppuk koḷḷātu tamiḻukku ākkan tēṭalām.
***immēṭaiyil araciyal pēcappaṭṭatu; puraṭci pēcappaṭṭatu;  camaya vaḷarcci pēcappaṭṭatu.  avaikaḷait taḻuvic cilavurai pakara virumpukiṟēṉ.  periyapurāṇam aṉpārnta araciyalnūl; aṟappuraṭci nūl;  camayam vaḷarkkum caṉmārkka nūl.  annūl mūvar varalāṟukaḷaic ciṟappākac colkiṟatu.  camaya vaḷarccikku araciyal atikāram iṉṟiyamaiyātatu eṉṟa appurāṇam terivikkiṟatu.  appar varalāṟṟilum campantar varalāṟṟilum puraṭcikaḷ maruvukiṉṟaṉa.  appar nikaḻttiya puraṭci oruvitam.  campantar nikaḻttiya puraṭci vēṟuvitam.  iraṇṭum muṟaiyē pallava maṉṉaṉaiyum, pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉaiyum aṭiyava
rākkiṉa.  avviruvar puraṭciyāl camayam vaḷarnta tiṟattai viḷakku
vatu cuntarar varalāṟu.  cuntaramūrtti cuvāmikaḷ tirupparaṅkuṉṟattil eḻuntaruḷiyapōtu avar1 aṭiyil mūvēntar muṭikaḷuñ cāyntamaiyaip purāṇam pōṟṟukiṟatu.  ataṉ nuṭpameṉṉai?  tamiḻnāṭṭil mūvēntar oṉṟiya maṉamuṭaiyarāyc camayam vaḷarttamai pulappaṭukiṟatu.  ituvē nuṭpam.

kaḷiṉ payaṉum pukuntiruttal uṇaraṟpālatu.

mēlum āṭal neṟiyaippaṟṟi yāṉ pēciyataṉ cāram varumāṟu:
ñāṉacampantarai muttamiḻ virakar eṉṟu nām pōṟṟukiṟōm;  pōṟṟum aḷavil niṉṟuviṭukiṟōm.  avaratu muttamiḻ vāḻkkaiyil mukiḻkkumāṟu nām naṭakkiṉṟōmā?  illai eṉṟē colvēṉ.  pulavarkaḷ campantariṉ tamiḻai iyalaḷavil nōkkiviṭukiṟārkaḷ;  icait tamiḻ mītum nāṭakat tamiḻmītum kavalai celuttukiṟār
kaḷillai.  icaiyum nāṭakamum ñāṉacampantar pāṭṭi
luṇṭō eṉṟu cilar aiyuṟum nilaiyum nērntuḷḷatu.
ñāṉacampantar tamiḻp pāṭalkaḷellām iṟaivaṉaik kuṟikkoṇṭavai.  avaratu tamiḻp pāṭṭuk kōyilil nuḻaintu pārttāl iṟaivaṉiṉ mūṉṟu nilaikaḷ iṉitu viḷaṅkum.  oṉṟu iṟaivaṉatu vākku maṉaṅ kaṭanta nilai;  maṟṟoṉṟu avaṉ iyaṟkaiyai uṭalākkoṇṭa nilai;  iṉṉoṉṟu avaṉatu ōviya nilai.  campantar tiruppāṭṭuk
kaḷil ovvoṉṟu kāṭṭukiṟēṉ;

		tēlaritu nītipalavum

mutal nilaiyai uṉṉip pārppōm.  ētēṉum neñcil uṟukiṟatā?  niṟam uru illātatai eppaṭi niṉaippatu?  nītiyai eppaṭi niṉaippatu?
iraṇṭāvatu oruvāṟu uḷḷattil taṅkum.
mūṉṟāvatē uḷḷattil naṉṟākap paṭiyum.
mūṉṟāvatai uḷḷam niṉaintu niṉaintu paṇpaṭap paṭa iraṇṭāvatu teḷivākum; iraṇṭāvatai uṉṉa uṉṉa atu mutalāvatai uṇarttum.  aṭippaṭai mūṉṟāvatē.  atai viṭuttu mutalāvatai nāṭuvatu muṭavaṉ komput tēṉukku ācaippaṭṭa kataiyāka muṭiyum.
kaṅkai maticcaṭai muṭiyum, mukkaṇṇum, nīlakaṇṭamum, māṉ maḻuk kaiyum, pāmpaṇiyum, eṭutta poṟpātamum, ūṉṟiya pātamum eppaṭi neñciṟ paṭiyumeṉpatai viḷakka vēṇṭuvatillai.
maṉattil naṉku paṭiyavalla ōviyam kallil amaiyum;  collil amaiyum;  maṟṟumoṉṟilum amaiyum.  aḵtu etu?  aḵtu āṭal.  avvāṭaluṭaṉ pāṭaluñ cērntāl pulaṉkaḷil civamaṉṟō viḷaṅkum?  āṭalum pāṭalum maliya campantar iyal amaittamai cintikkat takkatu.  ataṉāl campantar muttamiḻ virakar eṉṟu pōṟṟappaṭṭār.
kuḻantaiyiṉ āṭalai nēṟṟum pārttōm;  iṉṟum pārttōm.  civattiṉ tiruvuruvam ōviyamākak kallil maṭṭum mukiḻppataṉṟu;  collil maṭṭum mukiḻppataṉṟu;  āṭa
lilum mukiḻppatu.  āṭalil civa ōviyam mukiḻkkumpōtu āṭuvōr civamākiṟār.  avartam eṉputōl maṟaikiṉṟaṉa.    ākavē āṭalum vaḻipāṭṭukkuriyateṉṟu terivittuk koḷkiṟēṉ.
nēṟṟuk kuḻantaiyiṉ āṭalil oru kūṟṟil naṭarāja ōviyam mukiḻttatu.  appoḻutu kuḻantai tillaik kūttaṉākiyatu.  ituvē kalai;  āṭaṟ kalai;  āṭal pāṭaṟ kalai.
ñāṉacampantariṉ tamiḻk kāvil āṭal mayil āṭukiṟatu;  pāṭal kuyil pāṭukiṟatu.  kaṇkāḷ kāṇmiṉkaḷō!  cevikāḷ kēṇmiṉkaḷō!

cūṭal neṟiniṉṟāṉ cūlañcēr kaiyiṉāṉ
āṭal neṟiniṉṟāṉ āmāttūr ammāṉṟaṉ
vēṭa neṟinillā vēṭamum vēṭamē.
ivvariya āṭaṟkalaiyaip pāḻpaṭuttiṉōm.  atai uyirppikkavēṇṭuvatu nam kaṭamai.
iruḷ cūḻnta innāḷil ikaḻ pukaḻ karutātu. tiyāka eriyil kutittu, āṭal tiṟattaik kākkavanta tōḻar taṇṭapāṇikkum, avartam arumai maṉaiviyārkkum eṉatu naṉṟi uriyatāka;  kuḻantai maṅkaiyarkkaracikku eṉatu vāḻttu uriyatāka.  vāḻka tamiḻ;  velka muttamiḻ.
kuṟṟālak kāṭci
maturaiyil pakal nērattil tiruvaḷḷuvar kaḻakam 
eṉakku nīḻal aḷittatu.  aṅkē cuppiramaṇiya tā, pālacuppiramaṇiyam mutaliya teṉkāci nēyarkaḷaik kaṇṭēṉ;  muttukkumāracāmip piḷḷai eṅkē eṉṟu kēṭṭēṉ.  varuvār eṉṟa patil kiṭaittatu.  yāṉ avaraik kaṇṭēṉillai.  maturaik kaḻakam eṉakkut teṉkācik kaḻakamākavē tōṉṟiyatu.  paṭuttukkoṇṭē niṉaivu ulakil kuṟṟālap pātaiyil naṭantēṉ;  malaiyēṟiṉēṉ;  aruviyil mūḻkiṉēṉ;  iyaṟkai veḷiyilē cōṟuṇṭēṉ;  iyaṟkai iṉpam nukarntēṉ.  maturaik kaḻakam kuṟṟālak kāṭci vaḻaṅkiyatu.
paḷḷik kōyil 
maturaiyai viṭuttuk kāraikkuṭi vaḻiyē 30.12.1943 amarāvatip puttūrai aṭaintēṉ.  aṅkē paḻaiya tōḻar co. murukappāviṉ viruntiṉaṉāy orunāḷ taṅkiṉēṉ;  avarāl naṭattappeṟum māṇṭicōrip paḷḷiyaiyum piṟa nilaiyaṅkaḷaiyum pārvaiyiṭṭēṉ.  paḷḷi eṉakku kōyi
lākak kāṭci yaḷittatu.  kōyil eṉṟāl aṅkē teyvam iruttal vēṇṭumaṉṟō?  teyvam irukkiṟatu;  et
teyvam?  kuḻantaimait teyvam.  teyvak kuḻantaikaḷiṉ āṭalkaḷai eṉ pulaṉkaḷ parukiṉa.  eṉ uḷḷam iṉpak kaṭalāṭiyatu.  murukappar vāḻkkai palatiṟat toṇṭu
kaḷālākiyatu.  avaikaḷil viḻumiyatu ippaḷḷit toṇṭu.  ittoṇṭu peruka;  vaḷarka;  ōṅka eṉṟu eṉatu  uḷḷatti
ṉiṉṟum poṅki vaḻinta aṉpu vāḻttiyatu.
eṇṇattiṉ āṟṟal
ceṭṭi nāṭṭilē amarāvatip puttūrilē oru kallūri eḻutal vēṇṭumeṉṟu yāṉ eṇṇiṉēṉ;  oru nāḷā eṇṇiṉēṉ?  paṉṉeṭu nāḷ eṇṇiṉēṉ.  ataṟkuriya muyaṟciyum murukappar mutaliyōr vāyilāka orupōtu matarttatu.  annērattil oruvitaccūṟāvaḷi vīci muyaṟcip poṅkarai vīḻttiyatu.  itukuṟittu muṉṉarum colli
yuḷḷēṉ.  cūṟāvaḷi, muyaṟcip poṅkarai vīḻttiyatu;  eṇṇattai vīḻttiyatō?  illai.  nalleṇṇam eṉṟā
tal ceyalātal orutalai.
muṟpiṟavi ūṟṟu
murukappar cēyc celvam peṟṟiruttalai immuṟai kaṇṭēṉ.  avar cēy vīra pāṇṭiyaṉ;  aintāṇṭuk kuḻavi.  avaṉ vaṭivil kuḻantai.  pēccilum karuttilum avaṉ piḷḷaimai eytiya oruvaṉāyirukkiṟāṉ.  avaṉ etaiyuṅ kiḷip piḷḷaiyaippōl oppuvikkiṉṟāṉillai;  uṇarcci
yuṭaṉ pēcukiṟāṉ.  avaṉpāl meyppāṭukaḷ arumpukiṉṟaṉa.  avaṉ maṉattil muṟpiṟaviūṟṟut tiṟantuḷḷatu.  atu curakkiṟatu.  murukappar illattil oru tiruñāṉacampantar ulavukiṟār eṉṟu karuti, vīrapāṇṭiyaṉai vāḻttik kāraik
kuṭi hintu matāpimāṉa caṅkattai naṇṇiṉēṉ.
veḷḷi viḻā
accaṅkattiṉ veḷḷi viḻā eṉ talaimaiyil (31.12.1943; 1.1.1944) naṭaipeṟṟatu. mutalil caṅka nilaiyattukkuk kālkōḷ viḻā nikaḻttiṉēṉ;  piṉṉē viḻāvai naṭāttiṉēṉ.  ellām veḷḷi vaṇṇamākak kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.
caṅka amaiccar paḻa. vēṅkaṭācalañ ceṭṭiyār vīṭu viruntaḷittatu;  aruṇācalañ ceṭṭiyār vīṭu uṟaiviṭa
māyiṟṟu.  taṇṇīrmalaiyāṉ ceṭṭiyār mutaliyōr mukamaṉ pōṟṟaṟkuriyatā yiruntatu.

tillaiyē.  ataiyum itaiyum niṉaintu niṉaintu ciṟitu mayaṅkiṉēṉ.  vaikaṟaiyil anta milā āṉantam aṇi
koḷtillai kaṇṭēṉē eṉṉum pāṭṭoli kēṭṭatu.  kaṇṇai viḻittup pārttēṉ.  rāya cokkaliṅkam pāṭu
kiṟār.  cokkaliṅkam icai ñāṉiyallar.  āṉāl avaratu kālaippāṭṭu eṉṉaik koṇṭatu.  ārvattuṭaṉ  eḻun

cokkaliṅkaṉār kāṅkira toṇṭāṟṟic ciṟai pukuntavar;  kāraikkuṭi (nakarac) capait talaivarāyiruntavar;  ippoḻutu pāṭṭilum ōviyattilum mūḻkukiṟār;  kalaiyil mūḻkukiṟār;  tamiḻ vakuppu naṭattukiṟār.  avar vāyilākac ceṭṭināṭu tamiḻk kalaiyil eḻucci peṟumeṉṉum uṟuti eṉakku ēṟpaṭṭatu.

āṉantam
eṉ āṉantam muṭivuraiyil veḷivantatu.  iṉṟaikku irupattaintāṇṭukku muṉṉar nām vitaitta vitai vaḷarntē varukiṟatu.  ceṭṭināṭu kalaināṭākum eṉṉum makiḻcci
yuṭaṉ tirucirāppaḷḷi cērntēṉ.
tiricci
tiricciyil aṉparum vīrarumākiya vicuvanātaṉ illattil taṅkiṉēṉ.  vīrar muyaṟciyāl ciṭṭi ameccuvar caṅkattiṉ cārpil (2.1.1944) īṇṭiya peruṅkūṭṭatil nakara
capai āṇaiyar pi.si. talaivar talaimaiyil tamiḻppaṟṟu eṉṟa poruḷpaṟṟip pēciṉēṉ.  aṅkiruntu aṭutta nāḷ tiruttavattuṟai eṉṉum lālkuṭikkup pōṉēṉ.  tiru
vaḷḷuvar kaḻakattil talaimai pūṇṭu, māṇākkar aḷitta vāḻttai ēṟṟuc ceṉṉai aṇaintēṉ.
maṇiviḻāvukkup piṉṉart tamiḻ nāṭṭil yāṉ eṉṉa kaṇṭēṉ?  moḻiyuṇarcci kaṇṭēṉ;  cāti mata uṇarvu, orumaip pāṭṭukkuk kēṭu cūḻtalaiyuṅ kaṇṭēṉ;  orumaip
pāṭṭaik kalaiyāl uṇṭupaṇṇutal kūṭumeṉṟu uṇarkiṟēṉ.
antaṇmai
eṉ vāḻkkaik kuṟippukkaḷ cilavaṟṟaip potu makkaḷ muṉṉē kiṭattiyuḷḷēṉ;  iṉṉuñ cila kuṟippukkaḷ eṉ karuviluḷḷaṉa.  kākita nerukkut tīrnta piṉṉai avaikaḷum veḷivaralām.
palatiṟat tollaikaḷiṭai eṉ vāḻkkai vaḷarntatu;  ataṉāl atu poruṭ perukkil puraḷātatāyiṟṟu;  toṇ
ṭiṉ perukkil puraḷvatāyiṟṟu.  vāḻkkaiyiṉ aṭaivu poruṭperukkāyiṉ eṉṉuṭaiyatu tōlvi eytiyatākum;  toṇṭāyiṉ atu veṟṟi eytiyatākum.
toṇṭiṉ cēy etu?  antaṇmai.  toṇṭu, antaṇmai ūṟṟait tiṟantatu.  antaṇmai aruviyāy ōṭukiṟatu.  atu veḷḷamātal vēṇṭum.  eṉatu vāḻkkai orōvaḻi antaṇac celvattaip peṟṟatu.  avvantaṇac celvam muḻunilai eytum muyaṟciyil eṉatu vāḻkkaiyiṉ eñciya pakutiyum īṭupaṭumāṟu āṇṭavaṉ aruḷ kāppāṉāka.
ippiṟaviyil eṉatu vāḻkkai antaṇac celvam peṟutaṟkut tuṇai niṉṟa kalvi kēḷvikaṭkum, nalliṇak
kattukkum, ilvāḻkkaikkum, iyakkaṅkaṭkum, piṟavaṟṟiṟkum eṉatu vāḻttu uriyatāka.
paramaṇṭalaṅkaḷilirukkiṟa eṅkaḷ pitāvē, ummuṭaiya nāmam paricuttappaṭuvatāka.
ummuṭaiya rājyam varuvatāka, ummuṭaiya cittam paramaṇṭalattilē ceyyappaṭukiṟatupōlap pūmiyilēyum ceyyappaṭuvatāka.
aṉṟaṉṟu vēṇṭiya eṅkaḷ ākārattai iṉṟum eṅkaḷukkut tārum.
eṅkaḷ kaṭaṉāḷikaḷukku nāṅkaḷ maṉṉikkiṟatu pōla eṅkaḷ kaṭaṉkaḷai eṅkaḷukku maṉṉiyum.
eṅkaḷaic cōtaṉaikkuṭpaṭap paṇṇāmal tīyōṉiṉiṉṟu eṅkaḷai iraṭciyum.  rājyamum, vallamaiyum, makimaiyum, eṉṟeṉṟaikkum ummuṭaiya vaikaḷē

pāṭacālaiyil cērtal.  1894 veli kalācālaiyil cērtal.  cila mātaṅkaṭkuḷ pattiya muṟivāl kāl kaikaḷ muṭaṅkutal.  1894-1898 paṭippukku ōyvu.  1898 mīṇṭum veli kalācālaiyil cērtal.  ṭicampar 1901 katiraivēṟ piḷḷaiyavarkaḷai mutaṉ mutal cantittal.  1901-1906 katiraivēlariṉ māṇākkarāyp payiṟci peṟutal.  1903 srī pālacuppiramaṇiya paktajaṉa capai toṭaṅkutal.  1904 katiraivēṟ piḷḷai vaḻakkil cāṉṟu kūṟutal, meṟṟi kulēṣaṉ parīkṣaikkuc cellat tērnteṭukkappaṭāmai, paḷḷikkūṭap paṭippiṉ muṭivu.  11-9-1905 tantaiyār civapata maṭaital.  1906 pukkīppiṅ payiṟci peṟṟut tēṟutal.  26-3-1907 katiraivēṟ piḷḷai kantaṉaṭi cērtal.  1907 kaṭaṟkaraiyil vipiṉa cantirapālar coṟpoḻivukaḷaik kēṭṭal.  1907-1908 peṉsar kampeṉiyil vēlaipārttal.  1908 katiraivēṟ piḷḷai caritam iyaṟṟal.  1908-1910 mayilai makā vitvāṉ taṇikācala mutaliyāriṭam cittānta cāttiram payilutal.  1908-1910 umāpati kuruppirakācam pira toṭaṅkip periya purāṇa urai veḷiyiṭal.  1908-1915 tiruvallikkēṇic civaṉaṭiyār tirukkūṭṭac cārpil periyapurāṇam mutaṟ kāṇṭam piracaṅkam ceytu muṭittal, 1908 jaṭi sar ṭi. catāciva jayaravarkaḷ naṭpup peṟutal.  1910 aṉṉipesaṇṭ ammaiyāraic cantittal.  1910-1916 āyiram viḷakkup paḷḷik kūṭattil āciriyarāyp paṇipurital.  ṭicampar 1911 caiva cittānta makā camājattil mutaṉ mutal coṟpoḻi vāṟṟutal.  13-9-1912 kamalāmpikaiyammaiyārait tirumaṇam purital.  1912-1918 illaṟam naṭattutal.  ṭicampar 1912 kāñci purattil maṇam pūṇṭi kumāracāmi piḷḷaiyiṉ naṭpuk peṟutal.  1913 paṭṭiṉattār pāṭalkaṭku urai eḻutal.  ṭicampar 1913 srīlasrī ñāṉiyār cuvāmikaḷ talaimaiyil caivacittānta makācamāja viḻāvil pēcutal.  1914 cupparāyakāmat cantippu.  1914 e. srīnivāsa aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ naṭpu.  1915 putuppākkam civacuppiramaṇiya paktajaṉa capaiyiṉ tōṟṟam. jūṉ 1916 veli kallūriyil talaimait tamiḻāciriyarātal.  ṭicampar 1916 jaṭi kaṭciyiṉ piṟappu.  1917 kāñcipurattil vāṭiyāviṉ kēṇmai.  ceṭmpar 1917 araciyaṟṟuṟaiyil kaṉṉip pēccu.  cila nāṭkaḷukkuḷ ceṉṉai mākāṇac caṅkattiṉ piṟappu.  navampar 1917 mēṟpaṭi caṅkak kāriyataricikaḷuḷ oruvarātal.  ṭākṭar varatarājalu nāyuṭuvaik kāṇṭal.  05-12-1917 veli kallūrit tamiḻāciriyar pataviyiliruntu vilakutal.  7-12-1917 tēcapaktaṉ pattirikāciriya rātal.  8-12-1917 cakkaravartti irāja kōpālācāriyāravar kaḷai mutaṉ mutal kāṇṭal.  27-4-1918 ceṉṉait toḻi lāḷar caṅkam tōṉṟutal.  18-9-1918 maṉaiviyār kamalāmpikai yammaiyār civapatamaṭaital.  28-2-1919 tēcapaktaṉ īṭukāṇam paṟimutal peyyappaṭutal.  18-3-1919 makātmā kāntiyai mutaṉ mutal pārttal.  6-4-1919 cattiyākkiraka nōṉpu nāḷ.  12-6-1919 lārṭ villiṅṭaṉ tēciyap pattirikāciriyarkaṭku eccarikkai vaḻaṅkutal.  1919 ī.vē. irāmacāmi nāyakkar naṭpup peṟutal.  17-12-1919 pālakaṅkātara tilakar kāṭci.  11-1-1920 aṉṉi pesaṇṭ ammaiyārukku varavēṟpu.  4-7-1920 mattiyat toḻi lāḷar caṅkam piṟattal.  22-7-1920 tēcapaktaṉ āciriyar patavi nīttal.  21-10-1920 pakkiṅkāmmil katavaṭaippu.  22-10-1920 navacakti pattirikai toṭaṅkutal.  11-11-1920 toḻilāḷar iyakkat talaivarmītu vaḻakku.  1920-1929 pala araciyal makānāṭukaḷil talaimai vakittalum paṅku peṟutalum.  27-1-1921 mil katavaṭaippiṉ muṭivu.  20-6-1921 mīṇṭum vēlai niṟuttam.  5-7-1921 larṭ villiṅṭaṉuṭaṉ cantippu.  9-8-1921 vaḻakku vāpī vāṅkappaṭutal.  10-8-1921 eyliṅ kamiṭṭi vicāraṇai.  29-8-1921 tuppākkip pirayōkam.  ṭicampar 1921 vēlai niṟutta muṭivu.  21, 22-11-1925 kāñcipurattil tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kāṅkira makānāṭṭut talaimai, vakuppuvārip piratinitittuvam paṟṟi tīrmāṉam nirākarikkappaṭutal, ī.ve.rā. mutaliyōr veḷiyēṟṟam.  1926 teṉ intiya jīvarakṣā capaiyiṉ piṟappu.  25-2-1927 cāpūrji caklatvālā cantippu.  9-10-1928 tamaiyaṉār putalvaṉ 1	toḻilāḷar caṅkaṅkaḷ āṅkāṅkē nāṭōṟun tōṉṟivarukiṉṟaṉa.  avaikaḷai vaḻituṟaiyillāmal vaḷarttu varuvatāl, pala iṭarkaḷ iṭaiyil nērntuviṭum.  ātalāl ellāt toḻiṟcaṅkaṅkaṭkum naṭunāyakamāka oru mattiyac caṅkamiruttal vēṇṭum.  accaṅkattil ellāt toḻiṟcaṅkaṅkaḷuñ cērtal vēṇṭum.  mattiyac caṅkam oṉṟiruppatu toḻilāḷar iyakkattukku ākkantēṭuvatākum.  oru mattiyac caṅkam amaikka srīmati mirunāḷiṉi caṭṭōpāttiyāyā ceytuvarum muyaṟcikku immakānāṭu tuṇaiceyyumeṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ.
1	To
	(KALYANASUNDARA MUDALIAR, Esq, Madras.)
	My Dear Friend,
	     This is your book as much as mine.  If the speeches delivered here achieved any good among out friends at perambur it is due to your excellent translations of them.  What could I have done in the Labour work in this city without you?  you translated my speeches - not my words merely but their very spirit.  We have rejoiced and suffered together with thousands.  Your unobtrusive manner, your loyal support, your sagacious advice have been priceless.  Let me request you to accept this small gift of dedication for your all too splendid services.
	Ever Yours.
	B.P. Wadia.
	     (‘Labour in Madras’ by B.P. Wadia with a foreword by Col. Josiah. 
C. Wedgwood, M.P. (1921.) S. Ganesan & Co., Publishers, Triplicane.S.E.)
1	All these parties were present on the 10th August except the Madras Labour Union President who addressed the Committee the following letter:-
	‘Sir,
	I have the honour to acknowledge the receipt of your letter dated the 6th inst, relating to the sittings of the committee of enquity into the Madras Disturbances of June and July 1921 and to thank you for inviting me to be present at the said enquiry.
	In reply I am desired by the Madras Labour Union to inform you that the Madras Labour Union as such does not propose to participate in the enquiry, which as the Government order points out, will not extend to the merits and to any disputes between the management of the Buckingham and Carnatic mills and their employees.  The enquiry into the disturbances which took place in the perambur division on and after the 29th June, is a matter of concern to the general public and not to the members of the union as such, and it affects the peace and order of the northern parts of the city.  The Labour Union repudiates any manner of connection with such disturbances as have taken place.  They have since been in part at least a matter of adjudication before the magisterial courts in Madras and evidence has been adduced before these courts at the instance of the police as well as the parties aggrieved and judgements have been pronounced by the Chief Presidency Magistrate of the city in two cases which show clearly the origin of the disturbance and the persons involved in provoking them as well as the conduct of the police in dealing with them.  There are still a number of police proceedings pending before stipendiary and honorary magistrates in respect of this matter in which members of the union are being needlessly harassed and put to great hardship.  In the circumstances, the union is unable to furnish the committee with a list of persons who might be examined as witnessess by the committee and I regret very much to find myself unable to supply you now or later with a list of persons whom I could in the existing state of things suggest for examination as witnessess.
	Yours truly,
	T.V. KALYANASUNDARAM
President, Madras Labour Union.
		   In consequence of this refusal on the part of the Labour Union to participate in the enquiry which was much regretted by the committee announced at the first meeting that they desired it to be known that any accredited representatives of the strikers would by afforded the same facilities in regard to this enquiry, as would have been given to the Madras Labour Union.  The representatives of the Press were asked to give every possible publicity to this announcement.  But unfortunately no representative of the strikers or any section of them appeared during the inquiry...”
	Hindu (14.10.1921)

WELCOME ADDRESS
TO
Sriman T.V. KALYANASUNDARA MUDALIAR,
Madras
1.	Dear Sir, 
		     We, the Chairman and the members of the Municipal Council, Vellore, have great pleasure in extending to you a heartly welcome in our midst this morning.
	     We have been closely watching with keen interest and sympathy your varied public activites since several years past and our firm conviction continues to be that you are a patriot of the first order and that to whatever channels you devote your energies and enthusiasm, you have in mind the national regeneration of our Motherland as your goal.
	     We remember with gratitude the eminent services you have done in the past with great personal sacrifice in strengthening the national movement of the Country and your then eloquent and lucid public utterances in our mother - tongue still ring in our ears as masterful contribution to the Tamil literature to be read and re-read and emulated by every well-wisher of the Country.
	     Your devotion to the cause of the poor and toiling millions is unrivalled.  You have long been connected with the Trade Union movement in the Presidency and it is, we believe, no exaggeration to say that the status and 
	the position which this body has now gained is due largely to the sustained valuable services rendered by you with unabated enthusiasm and love towards that cause.  The mantle of the Presidentship of the District Labour Conference which begins its sessions to - day has therefore rightly fallen on 
	your shoulders, and we hope that under your guidance the Conference will have an eventful success this year.
	     In these days of degeneration of our mother-tongue we rejoice to find in you a Tamil scholar of the first rank with rare powers of exposition of our classical literature.  With your facile pen and gift of expression, you have been doing invaluable services to the Country in bringing home to the youths the beauties of our ancient language.
	May the Almighty grant you many more years of healthy and prosperous life.
	We remain, Dear Sir,
	Yours sincerely.
	Vellore	Chairman& Members of the Municipal Council
	10.9.1933 }	Vellore.
2.	Messrs. T.V. KALYANASUNDARA MUDALIAR, Avl.,
		Journalist, Madras
AND
V.V.GIRI Avargal, Barrister at law, Madras.
	Dear Sirs.
	     We deem it a great honour to welcome you to this District and present you with an address on behalf of the District Board.  Both of you are so intimately connected with the development of the Trade Union Movement in this Presidency, that it is in the fitness of things that the first of you have been chosen for the honour of presiding over and that the second of you for opening the labour conference today.
	     You, Mr. Kalyanasundara Mudaliar, have had the unique privilege of being one of the pioneers of the labour movement of this country and one who have been prominently connected with all national movement of this presidency.  Your transparent patriotism and selfless devotion to the cause of the poor have won for you a name, which conduces ideas of piety, sacrifice, devotion and service in the minds of the several millions of the Tamil land.  Your learning and research have won for you a permanent place in the rich of Tamil Literature.
	     In spite of your poor health, you have never missed an opportunity to popularise and spread the cause of Tamil and of the truths contained in its
	sacred books.  As the Editor of Desa Bakthan and Navasakthi you have indicated the cause of nationalism with unfringing courage and devotion.  It is a matter of Peculiar gratification that we  can  count  you  as  one  of  our
	District men in view of the very many intimate connections of interest and men that you have in this district. 
	     May Almighty give you long life and good health to continue the cause which you have espoused on behalf of the poor and the mother land. ***
vellore	the president & members of the
10th September 1933 }	district BOARD, North Arcot.

1	I have much pleasure in giving this testimonial to Mr. T.V. Kalyanasundara Mudaliar with regard to his scholarship in Tamil.  On three occasion.  I prdesided at the lectures given by him on Tamil literature and I was struck by the extensive knowledge of that language which he desplayed on those occasions.  He has in him combined the extensive knowledge of a pandit and 
	the systematized scholarship of a modern teacher.  He will I am sure be a valuable acquisition to any college that may require this services as a Teacher of Tamil.
	Thottakkadu House. Madras	T. RAMAKRISHNA PILLAI.
	29.3.1916.
